# Agent Sandbox VM (D53c) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Give the agent a per-run Linux desktop: a Firecracker microVM in an unprivileged Compose service. Browser runs stay unchanged, and every D44 invariant holds by structure.

**Architecture:**

- **Slot.** A new slot kind, `desktop`, runs one Firecracker VM per Compose service.
- **Guest.** The guest is built from the browser-slot Dockerfile lineage. It is snapshotted before n.eko, Chromium and `deskd` start, and it is reset from the host by SIGKILL plus a restore.
- **Agent.** The agent reaches each slot through a formal `SlotProvider` seam (CDP as today, `deskd` over a vsock relay, a control API).
- **Egress.** Egress leaves the guest only through our own CONNECT proxy, which sits in the container's network namespace and imports navigation-scope and private-range rules from the new `packages/net-policy`.
- **Phases.** The work runs in five phases. P0 is a hard go/no-go gate. P1 proves browser parity before any desktop feature exists.

**Tech Stack:**

- Firecracker (Apache-2.0; release and sha256 pinned by P0), Linux 6.1 LTS guest kernel, Debian trixie guest from `ghcr.io/m1k1o/neko/chromium:3.1.6`, nftables.
- TypeScript on Node 24 (type stripping), zod 4.6.5, tldts 7.4.16, drizzle 0.45.3 and postgres.js 3.4.9, Vitest 5.0.3.
- Python 3 (stdlib, plus Debian `python3-xlib` and `python3-pyatspi`) for `deskd` only.
- Next.js 16 for the UI, Playwright 1.63, pnpm 10.34.6.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-agent-vm-design.md`. Section numbers (§N) refer to it. Research: `orchestration/runs/2026-10-09-06-research-agent-vm/report.md` ([R §N]). Decisions: D44, D45, D48, D50, D51, D52 and D53 in `orchestration/STATE.md`. `CLAUDE.md` is binding. Guard integration: §6 of `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-09-observer-design.md`.

## Global Constraints

- **The P0 gate is hard.** No task numbered P1.x or higher starts until `orchestration/runs/<date>-agent-vm-p0/report.md` exists with `verdict: go` in its front matter **and** the user has accepted it. A `no-go` stops this plan, and the orchestrator reports to the user.
- **P1 before P3.** No desktop feature (P3) merges before the P1 parity gate (Task P1.15) passes on the host.
- **Hosting:** one Docker Compose app on Dokploy. Desktop services sit behind the compose profile `desktop`.
- **Cost:** OpenAI is the only paid service. No hosted sandbox, no cloud VM, no Squid or other proxy product, no paid OCR.
- **Desktop slot container (exact):**
  - `devices: [/dev/kvm, /dev/net/tun]`;
  - `cap_drop: [ALL]`, `cap_add: [NET_ADMIN, SETUID, SETGID]` (NET_ADMIN is the only capability added beyond Docker's default set);
  - `read_only: true`, `security_opt: [no-new-privileges:true]`, `init: true`;
  - `mem_limit: 10g`, `memswap_limit: 10g`, `cpus: 4`, `pids_limit: 256`.

  No jailer, no `privileged`, no host install, no `daemon.json` change, no AppArmor profile.
- **Guest VM (exact):**
  - 2 vCPUs, 4096 MiB, 1280×800@30;
  - tap `192.168.127.1/30` (host) and `192.168.127.2/30` (guest), guest vsock CID 3;
  - vsock ports: 51 (`vm-init` start line), 52 (`deskd`), host 1025 (`golden`/`ready` lines).
- **Ports (exact):**
  - 9223 CDP, 9224 idle probe, 8080 n.eko, 4713 Pulse (DNAT to the guest);
  - 9225 deskd relay, 9226 control API (agent IP only);
  - 3128 proxy on the tap gateway;
  - `DESKTOP_MEDIA_PORT_BASE = 59100` (`desktop-N` → `59100 + N`).
- **Slot names:** `^(browser|desktop)-[1-9][0-9]?$`. `BROWSER_SLOTS` lists only `browser-*`, and `DESKTOP_SLOTS` lists only `desktop-*` (it may be empty).
- **Migrations:** `packages/db/migrations/00NN_desktop_slots.sql` (P1) and `00NN_desktop_mode.sql` (P3). Observer owns 0015, and the notes plan takes 0016 and up. **The NN numbers are assigned at merge time:** rename each file to the next free number and regenerate its journal entry and snapshot with `pnpm --filter @mastertutor/db generate`. Never reuse a number.
- **No copying of rules (principle 6):** `registrableDomain`, `sameSite`, `inRunScope`, `allowsTopLevel`, `V4_BLOCKS`, `isPrivateAddress`, `PrivateHostCheck` and `isFixtureHost` exist only in `packages/net-policy` after Task P2.1.
- **Security:**
  - Secrets never reach the model, logs, traces or screenshots.
  - `shell` command text, its output, file contents, URLs beyond registrable domain and page text never go into telemetry or logs.
  - Every `deskd` answer is untrusted, and is validated with zod at the agent.
- **Host:** read-only SSH checks are allowed in P0 (spec §20 H1). Running `/dev/kvm` containers on `coursebite` needs one user approval (H2) before Task P0.2. A host permission change (H3) needs its own approval. SMT and KSM are never changed (H5). Use only label-scoped cleanup (`mastertutor.ci=1`) and unique project names (D45).
- **Tests run on the Dokploy host** via `scripts/remote-test.sh <suite>` (D48). Never loosen a timeout, retry or assertion to get green.
- **No credentials** in any file, plan, commit or log. Never read `.env` files.
- **Pending decisions** (spec §21 (a)–(d)) are implemented as written. If the user changes one, the owning task is amended before it runs: (a) Task P3.7, (b) Task P3.9, (c) Tasks P3.1 and P3.10, (d) Task P3.4.
- **Commits:** one per task, with pathspecs only (`git commit -- <paths>`). Never `git add -A` and never stash: other agents share the worktree.

## Review Focus

- **A run whose kind has no idle slot.** A queued desktop run must not block browser runs, and the reverse (pinned in Task P1.5).
- **The guest reports lies after taint.** A `deskd` answer that is malformed, oversized or names a path outside `/home/neko` must be rejected at the agent, never trusted (pinned in Tasks P1.14 and P3.6).
- **DNS rebinding and raw IPs through the proxy.** A host that resolves public on the first lookup and private on the second, or a CONNECT to a literal private IP, IPv6-mapped IPv4 or decimal-encoded IP, must be refused (pinned in Tasks P1.8 and P2.3).
- **A reset while a run is mid-exec, or a kill during a long `shell` call.** The VMM must be gone within 1 s, and the next lease must see none of the old files (pinned in Tasks P1.10 and P4.2).
- **Bypass plus desktop.** Bypass must auto-approve `exec` and `file_export`, but never lift the proxy's private-range check or load vault sessions (pinned in Tasks P3.1, P3.7 and P4.1).

## Module dependency map (no cycles)

```
packages/net-policy  (tldts + node built-ins only)
packages/contracts   (adds desk.ts; unchanged deps)
apps/desktop-host  ──▶ net-policy, contracts
apps/agent         ──▶ net-policy, contracts, db, telemetry   (new dir src/desk/ ──▶ browser/, tools/; never reversed)
apps/web           ──▶ contracts, db
```

# Phase P0: Spike (hard gate)

P0 code lives on branch `vm-p0-spike` under `spikes/agent-vm/` and is **never merged**. Only the report is kept, as `orchestration/runs/<date>-agent-vm-p0/report.md`. Every measurement step records its raw output in `spikes/agent-vm/out/` and copies it into the report.

## Task P0.1: Read-only host checks

**Files:**
- Create: `spikes/agent-vm/host-check.sh`
- Create: `spikes/agent-vm/out/host-check.txt` (output)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the check script.** Every command is read-only.

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Read-only facts for the D53c spike (spec §20 H1). Changes nothing on the host.
set -uo pipefail
section() { printf '\n## %s\n' "$1"; }
section kernel;        uname -r
section kvm;           ls -l /dev/kvm; getent group kvm; stat -c '%g %a' /dev/kvm
section tun;           ls -l /dev/net/tun
section cpu-vulns;     grep -H . /sys/devices/system/cpu/vulnerabilities/* 2>/dev/null
section smt;           cat /sys/devices/system/cpu/smt/control 2>/dev/null
section ksm;           cat /sys/kernel/mm/ksm/run 2>/dev/null
section swap;          swapon --show; free -g
section cgroup;        stat -fc %T /sys/fs/cgroup; cat /sys/fs/cgroup/cgroup.controllers
section docker;        docker info --format '{{.ServerVersion}} {{.CgroupDriver}} {{.CgroupVersion}} {{.DockerRootDir}}'
section disk;          df -h "$(docker info --format '{{.DockerRootDir}}')"
section nft;           command -v nft || echo "nft not on host (not needed: it runs in the container)"
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it over SSH (H1, allowed by the brief).**

Run: `ssh coursebite-build 'bash -s' < spikes/agent-vm/host-check.sh | tee spikes/agent-vm/out/host-check.txt`
Expected: a cgroup v2 host, `/dev/kvm` present with group `kvm` and mode `660`, a numeric kvm GID.

- [ ] **Step 3: Decide early no-go conditions.**
  - `/dev/kvm` is absent: **no-go**, and stop.
  - `/dev/kvm` is mode 600 (root only): the report asks the user for H3. Stop until they answer.
  - cgroup v1, or `memory` is absent from `cgroup.controllers`: record "memswap_limit unverifiable" as a risk.

  Record the kvm GID as `KVM_GID` in the report.

- [ ] **Step 4: Ask the user for H2.** The question: "May the spike run containers with `/dev/kvm` and `/dev/net/tun` on coursebite (label `mastertutor.ci=1`, project `mt-vm-spike-*`, removed after each run)?" Wait for a yes before Task P0.2.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit** (spike branch): `git commit -m "spike(vm): read-only host checks" -- spikes/agent-vm/host-check.sh spikes/agent-vm/out/host-check.txt`

## Task P0.2: Firecracker boots in an unprivileged container

**Files:**
- Create: `spikes/agent-vm/fc/Dockerfile`, `spikes/agent-vm/fc/compose.yml`, `spikes/agent-vm/fc/boot.sh`

- [ ] **Step 1: Pick and pin a Firecracker release.** Take the latest stable `firecracker-v1.*-x86_64.tgz` from `https://github.com/firecracker-microvm/firecracker/releases`. Note its version and sha256, and check that its release notes list VMGenID and full-snapshot support. Write both values into `spikes/agent-vm/fc/Dockerfile`.

```dockerfile
FROM debian:trixie-slim
ARG FIRECRACKER_VERSION
ARG FIRECRACKER_SHA256
RUN set -eux; apt-get update; apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends ca-certificates curl iproute2 util-linux socat; \
    curl -fsSLo /tmp/fc.tgz "https://github.com/firecracker-microvm/firecracker/releases/download/${FIRECRACKER_VERSION}/firecracker-${FIRECRACKER_VERSION}-x86_64.tgz"; \
    echo "${FIRECRACKER_SHA256}  /tmp/fc.tgz" | sha256sum -c -; \
    tar -xzf /tmp/fc.tgz -C /tmp; install -m 0755 /tmp/release-*/firecracker-* /usr/local/bin/firecracker; \
    useradd -u 10001 -M fc; rm -rf /tmp/* /var/lib/apt/lists/*
COPY boot.sh /usr/local/bin/boot.sh
ENTRYPOINT ["/usr/local/bin/boot.sh"]
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the spike compose with the exact production limits.**

```yaml
name: mt-vm-spike
services:
  fc:
    build: { context: ., args: { FIRECRACKER_VERSION: "${FIRECRACKER_VERSION:?}", FIRECRACKER_SHA256: "${FIRECRACKER_SHA256:?}" } }
    labels: { mastertutor.ci: "1" }
    devices: [/dev/kvm, /dev/net/tun]
    cap_drop: [ALL]
    cap_add: [NET_ADMIN, SETUID, SETGID]
    group_add: ["${KVM_GID:?}"]
    read_only: true
    init: true
    security_opt: [no-new-privileges:true]
    mem_limit: 10g
    memswap_limit: 10g
    cpus: 4
    pids_limit: 256
    tmpfs: ["/run/vm:size=6g,mode=0700,uid=10001", "/tmp:size=64m"]
    volumes: ["./images:/opt/vm:ro"]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Boot Firecracker's CI quickstart kernel and rootfs** (from the Firecracker getting-started guide, downloaded on the host into `spikes/agent-vm/fc/images/`). `boot.sh` runs the VMM as uid `fc` with `setpriv --reuid=10001 --regid=10001 --groups=$KVM_GID --inh-caps=-all --no-new-privs`, configures it over the API socket, and times `InstanceStart` until the serial console prints the login prompt.

Run: `scripts/remote-test.sh`-style sync, then on the host `KVM_GID=<gid> FIRECRACKER_VERSION=... FIRECRACKER_SHA256=... docker compose -p mt-vm-spike-$RANDOM up --build --abort-on-container-exit`
Expected: the guest reaches userspace. Record boot ms over 10 runs.

- [ ] **Step 4: Confirm the container is unprivileged.** In the running container, `grep Cap /proc/1/status` and `capsh --decode` show only `cap_net_admin,cap_setuid,cap_setgid`. `/proc/<firecracker pid>/status` shows `CapEff: 0000000000000000` and `Seccomp: 2`. Record both.

- [ ] **Step 5: Confirm `memswap_limit` is enforced.** `cat /sys/fs/cgroup/memory.swap.max` inside the container prints `0`. Record it.

- [ ] **Step 6: Clean up with labels only.** `docker compose -p <project> down -v`, then `docker image prune -f --filter label=mastertutor.ci=1`.

- [ ] **Step 7: Commit** (spike branch): `git commit -m "spike(vm): firecracker in an unprivileged container" -- spikes/agent-vm/fc spikes/agent-vm/out`

## Task P0.3: Guest from the slot lineage, snapshot, restore

**Files:**
- Create: `spikes/agent-vm/guest/Dockerfile` (FROM the `mastertutor/browser-slot:local` image built from `apps/browser-slot/Dockerfile`), `spikes/agent-vm/guest/vm-init`, `spikes/agent-vm/guest/build-rootfs.sh`, `spikes/agent-vm/guest/kernel.config`, `spikes/agent-vm/restore-bench.sh`

- [ ] **Step 1: Build a 6.1 LTS kernel.** Use Firecracker's `resources/guest_configs/microvm-kernel-ci-x86_64-6.1.config` plus this fragment. Record the tarball sha256.

```
CONFIG_VIRTIO_VSOCKETS=y
CONFIG_VHOST_VSOCK=n
CONFIG_VMGENID=y
CONFIG_OVERLAY_FS=y
CONFIG_TMPFS=y
CONFIG_MODULES=n
CONFIG_IPV6=n
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Build the rootfs.** Run `docker build` of the guest image, then `docker create`, `docker export | tar -x -C tree`, and `mkfs.ext4 -d tree -L mtguest rootfs.ext4`. Record the size.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the spike `vm-init`.** It mounts, puts an overlay upper on tmpfs, starts Xorg + openbox + PulseAudio, writes `golden` to host vsock 1025, then waits on `socat VSOCK-LISTEN:51` for `start <ms>`, sets the clock, and starts supervisord. Use the production text from Task P1.12 Step 5, so the spike tests the real thing.

- [ ] **Step 4: Measure.** `restore-bench.sh` takes one golden snapshot, then 30 times:
  1. SIGKILL the VMM.
  2. Spawn a new one.
  3. `PUT /snapshot/load` with a File mem backend and `resume_vm: true`.
  4. Send `start`.
  5. Poll `http://192.168.127.2:9222/json/version` from inside the container (spike only) until a new id answers.

  It reports p50 and p95 of (a) load-to-resumed ms and (b) load-to-new-CDP-id ms, and peak `memory.current`.

Run on the host. Expected (go thresholds): (a) p95 ≤ 500 ms; (b) p95 ≤ 5000 ms; peak memory ≤ 9 GiB.

- [ ] **Step 5: Verify the unknowns** [spec §5.4]. Record each result:
  - **vsock listener survival.** After restore, does `socat VSOCK-LISTEN:51` still accept? If not, switch the spike to guest-dials-host (vsock 1026) and record that P1.10 must use it.
  - **VMGenID.** Do `cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id` and the first 16 bytes of `/dev/urandom` read right after `start` differ across two restores? (They must.)
  - **Clock.** After `start`, `date +%s` in the guest is within 2 s of the host.
  - **No leftovers.** Write `/home/neko/leftover` in run 1, recycle, and check it is absent in run 2.

- [ ] **Step 6: Commit** (spike branch): `git commit -m "spike(vm): guest rootfs, golden snapshot, restore timing" -- spikes/agent-vm/guest spikes/agent-vm/restore-bench.sh spikes/agent-vm/out`

## Task P0.4: n.eko, WebRTC mux and CDP from the agent

**Files:**
- Create: `spikes/agent-vm/net/desktop.nft`, `spikes/agent-vm/net/compose.override.yml`, `spikes/agent-vm/net/cdp-probe.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the ingress DNAT.** Use the ruleset text from Task P1.13 Step 4: DNAT 9223, 9224, 8080 and 4713 plus the mux port to the guest. The spike guest needs no egress: the pages it shows are served inside the guest (Step 4).
- [ ] **Step 2: Probe CDP the way the agent does.** Run `cdp-probe.ts`, which imports `probeSlot` from `apps/agent/src/slots/probe.ts`, from a container on the spike's `cdp` network.

```ts
import { probeSlot } from "../../../apps/agent/src/slots/probe.ts";
const probe = await probeSlot("browser-1", { resolveHost: async () => process.env.SLOT_IP! });
console.log(JSON.stringify(probe));
```

Expected: `{ browser: "Chrome/…", protocolVersion: "1.3" }`. (The spike names its slot `browser-1` because `SlotName` does not accept `desktop-*` until P1.)

- [ ] **Step 3: Check the live view.** Run the stack with Traefik as `compose.test.yml` does, open `/live/<uuid>/`, and confirm that video starts over the TCP mux behind DNAT.
- [ ] **Step 4: Measure the stream.** Record n.eko fps (the client stats overlay) and the container's CPU (`docker stats --no-stream`) while scrolling a long page for 60 s. The page is served inside the guest by `python3 -m http.server` on guest loopback; the spike guest image installs `python3`.

Expected (go thresholds): ≥ 25 fps sustained; ≤ 1.5 CPUs.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit** (spike branch): `git commit -m "spike(vm): n.eko via DNAT and mux, CDP from the agent network" -- spikes/agent-vm/net spikes/agent-vm/out`

```ts
export const SLOT_NAME_PATTERN = /^(browser|desktop)-[1-9][0-9]?$/;
export const SlotName = z
  .string()
  .regex(SLOT_NAME_PATTERN, "Expected a slot name like browser-1 or desktop-1");
```

In `packages/contracts/src/enums.ts`, after `SlotState`:

```ts
/** D53c: a slot is a Chromium container (browser) or a Firecracker desktop VM (desktop). */
export const SLOT_KINDS = ["browser", "desktop"] as const;
export const SlotKind = z.enum(SLOT_KINDS);
export type SlotKind = z.infer<typeof SlotKind>;

/** D53c: where a run works, fixed at creation; a run leases only slots of its own kind. */
export const RUN_ENVIRONMENTS = SLOT_KINDS;
export const RunEnvironment = z.enum(RUN_ENVIRONMENTS);
export type RunEnvironment = z.infer<typeof RunEnvironment>;
```

In `packages/contracts/src/constants.ts`, replace `mediaPortForSlot` and the slot-list block:

```ts
import type { SlotKind } from "./enums.ts";

export const DESKTOP_MEDIA_PORT_BASE = 59100;
/** D53c: the deskd relay (TCP → vsock 52) and the desktop controller API; both admit the agent's IP only. */
export const DESK_PORT = 9225;
export const DESKTOP_CONTROL_PORT = 9226;

/** A slot's kind is its name's prefix (browser-N, desktop-N). */
export function slotKindOf(name: string): SlotKind {
  if (!SLOT_NAME_PATTERN.test(name)) throw new TypeError(`Invalid slot name: ${name}`);
  return name.startsWith("desktop-") ? "desktop" : "browser";
}

export function mediaPortForSlot(slot: string): number {
  const kind = slotKindOf(slot);
  const base = kind === "desktop" ? DESKTOP_MEDIA_PORT_BASE : MEDIA_PORT_BASE;
  return base + Number(slot.slice(kind.length + 1));
}

const slotListOf = (kind: SlotKind, min: number) =>
  z
    .string()
    .transform((value) =>
      value
        .split(",")
        .map((part) => part.trim())
        .filter((part) => part.length > 0),
    )
    .pipe(
      z
        .array(SlotName.refine((name) => slotKindOf(name) === kind, `Expected ${kind}-N slot names`))
        .min(min, "List at least one slot")
        .refine((names) => new Set(names).size === names.length, "Slot names must be unique"),
    );

/** BROWSER_SLOTS: comma-separated browser-N names, at least one. */
export const SlotList = slotListOf("browser", 1);
/** DESKTOP_SLOTS (D53c): comma-separated desktop-N names; empty means no desktop slots. */
export const DesktopSlotList = slotListOf("desktop", 0);
```

In `packages/contracts/src/env.ts`, import `DesktopSlotList`. Add to **both** `AgentEnv` and `MigrateEnv`, right after `BROWSER_SLOTS`:

```ts
  /** D53c: desktop VM slots (compose profile `desktop`); none unless listed. */
  DESKTOP_SLOTS: DesktopSlotList.default([]),
```

In `packages/contracts/src/telemetry.ts`, add `slotKind: "mt.slot.kind",` to `ATTR`, after `slotName`.

```ts
/** One row per Compose slot; rows are synced from BROWSER_SLOTS and DESKTOP_SLOTS by migrate. */
export const browserSlots = pgTable(
  "browser_slots",
  {
    name: text("name").primaryKey(),
    /** D53c: derived from the name's prefix, and held to it by a check. */
    kind: slotKindEnum("kind").notNull().default("browser"),
    state: slotStateEnum("state").notNull().default("restarting"),

    leaseOwner: text("lease_owner"),

    restartedAt: tstz("restarted_at"),
    createdAt: createdAt(),
  },

    check("browser_slots_name_valid", sql`${t.name} ~ ${sql.raw(`'${SLOT_NAME_PATTERN.source}'`)}`),
    check("browser_slots_kind_matches_name", sql`split_part(${t.name}, '-', 1) = ${t.kind}::text`),
  ],
);
```

In `packages/db/src/queries/slots.ts`:

```ts
import { SlotName, slotKindOf, type SlotState } from "@mastertutor/contracts";
// …
/** Makes browser_slots match BROWSER_SLOTS + DESKTOP_SLOTS: new slots start 'restarting' until the agent sees CDP. */
export async function syncBrowserSlots(
  sql: Sql | TransactionSql,
  names: readonly string[],
): Promise<void> {
  const valid = z
    .array(SlotName)
    .min(1)
    .parse([...names]);
  const rows = valid.map((name) => ({ name, kind: slotKindOf(name) }));
  await sql`insert into browser_slots ${sql(rows)} on conflict (name) do nothing`;
  await sql`delete from browser_slots
            where not (name = any(${sql.array(valid)})) and state <> 'leased'`;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the seam.** Create `apps/agent/src/slots/provider.ts`:

```ts
import { slotKindOf, type SlotKind } from "@mastertutor/contracts";

/**
 * D53c §9: the one seam between the agent and a slot of either kind. It formalises what SlotPool,
 * BrowserControl and cdpBaseUrl did separately. LiveView is not part of it: n.eko answers on
 * <slot>:8080 for both kinds.
 */
export interface SlotProvider {
  readonly kind: SlotKind;
  /** CDP base URL by IP (Chrome rejects hostnames in Host); both kinds answer on 9223. */
  cdpBaseUrl(name: string): Promise<string>;
  /** Changes on every fresh browser, and only then; null while unreachable. SlotPool's readiness signal. */
  readBrowserId(name: string): Promise<string | null>;
  /** Replaces the slot's browser with a fresh one holding nothing from before. */
  recycle(name: string): Promise<void>;
}

export interface SlotProviders {
  forSlot(name: string): SlotProvider;
}

export function slotProviders(byKind: {
  browser: SlotProvider;
  desktop?: SlotProvider;
}): SlotProviders {
  return {
    forSlot(name) {
      const kind = slotKindOf(name);
      const provider = kind === "browser" ? byKind.browser : byKind.desktop;
      if (!provider) throw new Error(`no provider for ${kind} slot ${name}`);
      return provider;
    },
  };
}
```

Create `apps/agent/src/slots/browser-provider.ts`:

```ts
import { cdpBrowserControl, type BrowserControl } from "./lifecycle.ts";
import { slotCdpBaseUrl } from "./probe.ts";
import type { SlotProvider } from "./provider.ts";

/** Browser slots: CDP Browser.close, after which the container exits and Compose restarts it. */
export function browserSlotProvider(
  options: { cdpBaseUrl?(name: string): Promise<string>; control?: BrowserControl } = {},
): SlotProvider {
  const baseUrl = options.cdpBaseUrl ?? ((name: string) => slotCdpBaseUrl(name));
  const control = options.control ?? cdpBrowserControl;
  const reachable = async (name: string) => {
    try {
      return await baseUrl(name);
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  };
  return {
    kind: "browser",
    cdpBaseUrl: baseUrl,
    async readBrowserId(name) {
      const url = await reachable(name);
      return url ? control.readBrowserId(url) : null;
    },
    async recycle(name) {
      const url = await reachable(name);
      if (url) await control.closeBrowser(url);
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
  /** Records the id of the browser a run attached to, so its replacement can be recognised. */
  async rememberBrowser(name: string): Promise<void> {
    const id = await this.#options.providers.forSlot(name).readBrowserId(name);
    if (id) this.#known.set(name, id);
  }
```

  - In `#reset`, add the kind to the span attributes: `{ [ATTR.slotName]: name, [ATTR.slotKind]: slotKindOf(name) }`.
  - In `#restart`, change only the three lines that touched CDP, so the loop reads:

```ts
    const provider = this.#options.providers.forSlot(name);
    while (Date.now() < deadline) {
      const id = await provider.readBrowserId(name);
      if (id) {
        if (neverLeased || (previous !== null && id !== previous)) {
          this.#known.set(name, id);
          if (await this.#options.store.markIdle(name)) this.#options.onIdle?.(name);
          return true;
        }
        if (!closedOnce) {
          await provider.recycle(name);
          closedOnce = true;
          previous = id;
        }
      }
      await delay(config.slotPollMs);
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** In `claim.ts`, import `slotKindOf` and `RunEnvironment` from contracts, `browserSlots` from db, and `count`/`ne` from drizzle. Before selecting the candidate:

```ts
    // D53c: only runs whose kind can lease now are candidates, so a run that must wait for its
    // kind never blocks runs of the other kind. Queued runs need two idle slots of their kind
    // (rule 3 warm reserve); a wake needs one. pickIdleSlot re-checks under SKIP LOCKED.
    const idleByKind = await tx
      .select({ kind: browserSlots.kind, idle: count() })
      .from(browserSlots)
      .where(and(eq(browserSlots.state, "idle"), inArray(browserSlots.name, [...options.slots])))
      .groupBy(browserSlots.kind);
    const kindsWith = (min: number): RunEnvironment[] =>
      idleByKind.filter((row) => row.idle >= min).map((row) => row.kind);
    const wakeKinds = kindsWith(1);
    if (wakeKinds.length === 0) return null;
    const queuedKinds = kindsWith(2);
    const leasable = or(
      and(ne(runs.status, "queued"), inArray(runs.environment, wakeKinds)),
      queuedKinds.length > 0
        ? and(eq(runs.status, "queued"), inArray(runs.environment, queuedKinds))
        : sql`false`,
    );
```

Add `leasable` to the candidate's `where(and(claimable, notKilled, leasable))`. Then call `pickIdleSlot` with only that kind's slots:

```ts
    const kindSlots = options.slots.filter((name) => slotKindOf(name) === candidate.environment);
    const slotName = await pickIdleSlot(tx, { slots: kindSlots, priority, concurrency });
```

**Files:**

  - `VmState { vmId: uuid | null }`, `RecycleResult { vmId: uuid, restoreMs }`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  DESK_METHODS,
  DESK_PARAMS,
  DESK_RESULTS,
  DeskPath,
  DeskRequestHeader,
  DeskResponseHeader,
  RecycleResult,
} from "./desk.ts";

describe("deskd wire (D53c §7)", () => {
  it("has params and results for exactly its methods", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(DESK_PARAMS).sort()).toEqual([...DESK_METHODS].sort());
    expect(Object.keys(DESK_RESULTS).sort()).toEqual([...DESK_METHODS].sort());
  });

  it("accepts only paths under /home/neko with no dot segments", () => {
    expect(DeskPath.safeParse("/home/neko/Documents/a.pdf").success).toBe(true);
    for (const bad of [
      "/home/neko/",
      "/home/neko/../root/x",
      "/home/neko/./a",
      "/etc/passwd",
      "/home/nekox/a",
      "/home/neko/a\0b",
      `/home/neko/${"a".repeat(600)}`,
    ])
      expect(DeskPath.safeParse(bad).success, bad).toBe(false);
  });

  it("caps payloads and rejects unknown methods and extra keys", () => {
    expect(DeskRequestHeader.safeParse({ id: 1, method: "hello", params: {}, bytes: 0 }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(DeskRequestHeader.safeParse({ id: 1, method: "exec", params: {}, bytes: 0 }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(
      DeskRequestHeader.safeParse({ id: 1, method: "hello", params: {}, bytes: 64 * 1024 * 1024 + 1 }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
    expect(
      DeskResponseHeader.safeParse({ id: 1, ok: true, result: {}, bytes: 0, extra: 1 }).success,
    ).toBe(false);
  });

  it("allows only short snake_case error codes", () => {
    const error = (code: string) =>
      DeskResponseHeader.safeParse({ id: 2, ok: false, error: { code, message: "x" }, bytes: 0 });
    expect(error("too_large").success).toBe(true);
    expect(error("Too Large").success).toBe(false);
  });

  it("bounds the controller's answers", () => {
    expect(RecycleResult.safeParse({ vmId: crypto.randomUUID(), restoreMs: 120 }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(RecycleResult.safeParse({ vmId: "x", restoreMs: 120 }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it and see it fail.**

Run: `scripts/remote-test.sh unit packages/contracts/src/desk.test.ts`
Expected: FAIL. The module does not exist.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `packages/contracts/src/desk.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";

/**
 * D53c §7: deskd, the agent's only helper inside the desktop guest (vsock 52, relayed on DESK_PORT).
 * Every answer comes from an untrusted guest: the agent parses each with these schemas. deskd's
 * `hello` must list exactly DESK_METHODS (a behaviour test checks it).
 */
export const DESK_METHODS = ["hello", "fileGet"] as const;
export const DeskMethod = z.enum(DESK_METHODS);
export type DeskMethod = z.infer<typeof DeskMethod>;

export const DESK_LIMITS = {
  headerBytes: 16_384,
  payloadBytes: 64 * 1024 * 1024,
  pathChars: 512,
} as const;

/** deskd reads and writes only below the desktop user's home (it re-checks after realpath). */
export const DESK_HOME = "/home/neko/";
export const DeskPath = z
  .string()
  .max(DESK_LIMITS.pathChars)
  .refine(
    (path) =>
      path.startsWith(DESK_HOME) &&
      path.length > DESK_HOME.length &&
      !path.includes("\0") &&
      !path.split("/").some((part) => part === "." || part === ".."),
    "Expected a path below /home/neko without . or .. segments",
  );

const Id = z.number().int().min(1).max(2_147_483_647);
const Bytes = z.number().int().min(0).max(DESK_LIMITS.payloadBytes);

export const DESK_PARAMS = {
  hello: z.strictObject({}),
  fileGet: z.strictObject({ path: DeskPath, maxBytes: Bytes.min(1) }),
} as const satisfies Record<DeskMethod, z.ZodType>;

export const DESK_RESULTS = {
  hello: z.strictObject({
    methods: z.array(z.string().max(32)).max(32),
    version: z.string().max(32),
  }),
  fileGet: z.strictObject({ size: Bytes }),
} as const satisfies Record<DeskMethod, z.ZodType>;

export const DeskRequestHeader = z.strictObject({
  id: Id,
  method: DeskMethod,
  params: z.unknown(),
  bytes: Bytes,
});
export type DeskRequestHeader = z.infer<typeof DeskRequestHeader>;

export const DeskError = z.strictObject({
  code: z.string().regex(/^[a-z][a-z_]{0,39}$/),
  message: z.string().max(200),
});
export type DeskError = z.infer<typeof DeskError>;

export const DeskResponseHeader = z.discriminatedUnion("ok", [
  z.strictObject({ id: Id, ok: z.literal(true), result: z.unknown(), bytes: Bytes }),
  z.strictObject({ id: Id, ok: z.literal(false), error: DeskError, bytes: z.literal(0) }),
]);
export type DeskResponseHeader = z.infer<typeof DeskResponseHeader>;

/** D53c §8.4: the desktop controller's API on DESKTOP_CONTROL_PORT (agent IP only). */
export const VmState = z.strictObject({ vmId: z.uuid().nullable() });
export type VmState = z.infer<typeof VmState>;
export const RecycleResult = z.strictObject({
  vmId: z.uuid(),
  restoreMs: z.number().int().min(0).max(600_000),
});
export type RecycleResult = z.infer<typeof RecycleResult>;
```

Add `export * from "./desk.ts";` to `packages/contracts/src/index.ts`.

```ts
export interface EgressTarget { origin: string; host: string; port: number }
export interface EgressScope { allowedOrigins: readonly string[]; tainted: boolean; learned: ReadonlySet<string> }
export type EgressDecision = "allowed" | "denied" | "private_blocked";
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  checkedAddress,
  cidrContains,
  parseAbsoluteTarget,
  parseConnectTarget,
  scopeAllows,
} from "./decide.ts";

const scope = (over: Partial<{ allowedOrigins: string[]; tainted: boolean; learned: Set<string> }> = {}) => ({
  allowedOrigins: ["https://learn.zybooks.com"],
  tainted: false,
  learned: new Set<string>(),
  ...over,
});

describe("egress targets", () => {
  it("parses CONNECT authorities and absolute http URLs only", () => {
    expect(parseConnectTarget("cdn.example.com:443")).toEqual({
      origin: "https://cdn.example.com", host: "cdn.example.com", port: 443,
    });
    expect(parseConnectTarget("example.com:8443")?.origin).toBe("https://example.com:8443");
    expect(parseConnectTarget("[::1]:443")?.host).toBe("::1");
    expect(parseConnectTarget("example.com")).toBeNull();
    expect(parseConnectTarget("example.com:22")).toBeNull(); // privileged non-web port
    expect(parseConnectTarget("example.com:70000")).toBeNull();
    expect(parseAbsoluteTarget("http://example.com/a?b")?.origin).toBe("http://example.com");
    expect(parseAbsoluteTarget("https://example.com/")).toBeNull(); // https goes through CONNECT
    expect(parseAbsoluteTarget("ftp://example.com/")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("scope (spec §8.3)", () => {
  it("allows every public site before taint", () => {
    expect(scopeAllows(parseConnectTarget("anything.example:443")!, scope())).toBe(true);
  });

  it("after taint allows the run scope, same-site hosts, and sites learned before taint", () => {
    const tainted = scope({ tainted: true, learned: new Set(["zycdn.net"]) });
    expect(scopeAllows(parseConnectTarget("static.zybooks.com:443")!, tainted)).toBe(true);
    expect(scopeAllows(parseConnectTarget("a.zycdn.net:443")!, tainted)).toBe(true);
    expect(scopeAllows(parseConnectTarget("evil.example:443")!, tainted)).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("private ranges (always)", () => {
  const resolver = (map: Record<string, string[]>) => async (host: string) => {
    const found = map[host];
    if (!found) throw new Error("ENOTFOUND");
    return found;
  };

  it("returns the address it checked, and refuses any private answer", async () => {
    expect(await checkedAddress("ok.example", resolver({ "ok.example": ["93.184.216.34"] }), [])).toBe("93.184.216.34");
    expect(await checkedAddress("mixed.example", resolver({ "mixed.example": ["93.184.216.34", "10.0.0.5"] }), [])).toBeNull();
    expect(await checkedAddress("169.254.169.254", resolver({}), [])).toBeNull();
    expect(await checkedAddress("::ffff:127.0.0.1", resolver({}), [])).toBeNull();
    expect(await checkedAddress("localhost", resolver({ localhost: ["127.0.0.1"] }), [])).toBeNull();
  });

  it("fails closed on lookup errors and empty answers", async () => {
    expect(await checkedAddress("nx.example", resolver({}), [])).toBeNull();
    expect(await checkedAddress("empty.example", resolver({ "empty.example": [] }), [])).toBeNull();
  });

  it("admits only the configured fixture CIDRs among private addresses", async () => {
    const fixtures = resolver({ "site.fixtures.test": ["172.30.240.20"] });
    expect(await checkedAddress("site.fixtures.test", fixtures, ["172.30.240.0/24"])).toBe("172.30.240.20");
    expect(await checkedAddress("site.fixtures.test", fixtures, [])).toBeNull();
    expect(cidrContains("172.30.240.0/24", "172.30.241.1")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("refuses inet_aton spellings once resolved (decimal, hex, short forms)", async () => {
    const aton = resolver({ "2130706433": ["127.0.0.1"], "0x7f.1": ["127.0.0.1"] });
    expect(await checkedAddress("2130706433", aton, [])).toBeNull();
    expect(await checkedAddress("0x7f.1", aton, [])).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `apps/desktop-host/src/egress/decide.ts`:

```ts
import { isIP } from "node:net";
import { inRunScope, isPrivateAddress, registrableDomain, type HostResolver } from "@mastertutor/net-policy";

/** D53c §8: one CONNECT or absolute-form request the guest asked the proxy for. */
export interface EgressTarget {
  origin: string;
  host: string;
  port: number;
}

export interface EgressScope {
  allowedOrigins: readonly string[];
  /** Set once the run executed code or acted on a native app; never cleared within a lease. */
  tainted: boolean;
  /** Sites the browser used before taint, so pages already open keep working. */
  learned: ReadonlySet<string>;
}

export type EgressDecision = "allowed" | "denied" | "private_blocked";

const webPort = (port: number) => port === 80 || port === 443 || (port >= 1024 && port <= 65_535);

function target(scheme: "http" | "https", host: string, port: number): EgressTarget | null {
  if (!host || !Number.isInteger(port) || !webPort(port)) return null;
  const bare = host.replace(/^\[|\]$/g, "").toLowerCase();
  const fallback = scheme === "https" ? 443 : 80;
  const shown = isIP(bare) === 6 ? `[${bare}]` : bare;
  return { origin: `${scheme}://${shown}${port === fallback ? "" : `:${port}`}`, host: bare, port };
}

/** "host:port" or "[v6]:port" from a CONNECT line. */
export function parseConnectTarget(authority: string): EgressTarget | null {
  const match = /^(\[[0-9a-fA-F:.]+\]|[A-Za-z0-9.-]{1,253}):([0-9]{1,5})$/.exec(authority);
  return match?.[1] && match[2] ? target("https", match[1], Number(match[2])) : null;
}

/** An absolute-form plain-http request line target; https always arrives as CONNECT. */
export function parseAbsoluteTarget(url: string): EgressTarget | null {
  let parsed: URL;
  try {
    parsed = new URL(url);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  if (parsed.protocol !== "http:" || parsed.username || parsed.password) return null;
  return target("http", parsed.hostname, parsed.port ? Number(parsed.port) : 80);
}

/** The registrable domain, or the exact host for IPs and bare suffixes (D51). */
export function siteOf(host: string): string {
  return registrableDomain(host) ?? host.toLowerCase();
}

/** Spec §8.3: untainted runs reach any public site; tainted runs only their scope and learned sites. */
export function scopeAllows(target: EgressTarget, scope: EgressScope): boolean {
  if (!scope.tainted) return true;
  return inRunScope(target.origin, scope.allowedOrigins) || scope.learned.has(siteOf(target.host));
}

const v4 = (ip: string) => ip.split(".").reduce((value, octet) => (value << 8) + Number(octet), 0) >>> 0;

export function cidrContains(cidr: string, ip: string): boolean {
  const [base, bitsText] = cidr.split("/");
  const bits = Number(bitsText);
  if (!base || isIP(base) !== 4 || isIP(ip) !== 4 || !(bits >= 0 && bits <= 32)) return false;
  const mask = bits === 0 ? 0 : (0xffffffff << (32 - bits)) >>> 0;
  return (v4(base) & mask) === (v4(ip) & mask);
}

/**
 * Resolves once and returns the address the proxy must connect to, or null. Every answer must be
 * public (or inside a test fixture CIDR); a lookup error or an empty answer fails closed. The
 * caller connects to exactly this address, so a second (rebinding) lookup never happens.
 */
export async function checkedAddress(
  host: string,
  resolve: HostResolver,
  allowCidrs: readonly string[],
): Promise<string | null> {
  let addresses: string[];
  try {
    addresses = isIP(host) ? [host] : await resolve(host);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  if (addresses.length === 0) return null;
  const fine = (address: string) =>
    !isPrivateAddress(address) || allowCidrs.some((cidr) => cidrContains(cidr, address));
  return addresses.every(fine) ? (addresses[0] ?? null) : null;
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { parseConnectTarget } from "./decide.ts";
import { EgressStateStore } from "./state.ts";

const t = (authority: string) => parseConnectTarget(authority)!;

describe("EgressStateStore", () => {
  it("learns sites before taint and reports each site's first use once", () => {
    const store = new EgressStateStore();
    expect(store.record(t("a.cdn.example:443"), "allowed")).toEqual({
      origin: "https://a.cdn.example", site: "cdn.example", decision: "allowed_first",
    });
    expect(store.record(t("b.cdn.example:443"), "allowed")).toBeNull();
    store.apply({ allowedOrigins: [], tainted: true });
    expect(store.scope().learned.has("cdn.example")).toBe(true);
    store.record(t("new.example:443"), "allowed");
    expect(store.scope().learned.has("new.example")).toBe(false); // nothing is learned after taint
  });

  it("never un-taints", () => {
    const store = new EgressStateStore();
    store.apply({ allowedOrigins: [], tainted: true });
    store.apply({ allowedOrigins: [], tainted: false });
    expect(store.scope().tainted).toBe(true);
  });

  it("reports every denial and private block", () => {
    const store = new EgressStateStore();
    expect(store.record(t("x.example:443"), "denied")?.decision).toBe("denied");
    expect(store.record(t("x.example:443"), "denied")?.decision).toBe("denied");
    expect(store.record(t("10.0.0.1:443"), "private_blocked")?.site).toBe("10.0.0.1");
  });
});
```

`apps/desktop-host/src/egress/proxy.test.ts` starts real local upstreams on loopback and treats `127.0.0.1` as a fixture CIDR, so private-range logic stays on for everything else:

```ts
import { once } from "node:events";
import { createServer, connect, type AddressInfo, type Server } from "node:net";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createEgressProxy } from "./proxy.ts";
import { EgressStateStore } from "./state.ts";

const servers: Server[] = [];
afterEach(async () => {
  await Promise.all(servers.splice(0).map((server) => new Promise((r) => server.close(r))));
});

async function listen(server: Server): Promise<number> {
  servers.push(server);
  server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1");
  await once(server, "listening");
  return (server.address() as AddressInfo).port;
}

/** An upstream that answers every connection with a fixed line, then closes. */
const upstream = (text: string) =>
  createServer((socket) => socket.end(`HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: ${text.length}\r\n\r\n${text}`));

async function ask(proxyPort: number, head: string): Promise<string> {
  const socket = connect(proxyPort, "127.0.0.1");
  socket.write(head);
  let text = "";
  socket.on("data", (chunk) => (text += chunk.toString("latin1")));
  await once(socket, "close");
  return text;
}

function proxyWith(map: Record<string, string[]>, store = new EgressStateStore()) {
  const notices: unknown[] = [];
  const proxy = createEgressProxy({
    state: store,
    resolve: async (host) => {
      const found = map[host];
      if (!found) throw new Error("ENOTFOUND");
      return found;
    },
    allowCidrs: ["127.0.0.1/32"],
    onNotice: (notice) => notices.push(notice),
  });
  return { proxy, notices, store };
}

describe("egress proxy", () => {
  it("tunnels CONNECT to the checked address", async () => {
    const port = await listen(upstream("hello"));
    const { proxy } = proxyWith({ "up.fixtures.test": ["127.0.0.1"] });
    const proxyPort = await listen(proxy);
    const answer = await ask(proxyPort, `CONNECT up.fixtures.test:${port} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: x\r\n\r\nGET / HTTP/1.1\r\n\r\n`);
    expect(answer).toMatch(/^HTTP\/1\.1 200 Connection Established\r\n\r\nHTTP\/1\.1 200 OK/);
    expect(answer).toMatch(/hello$/);
  });

  it("forwards absolute-form http in origin form with Connection: close", async () => {
    let seen = "";
    const port = await listen(
      createServer((socket) =>
        socket.once("data", (chunk) => {
          seen = chunk.toString("latin1");
          socket.end("HTTP/1.1 204 No Content\r\n\r\n");
        }),
      ),
    );
    const { proxy } = proxyWith({ "up.fixtures.test": ["127.0.0.1"] });
    const proxyPort = await listen(proxy);
    await ask(proxyPort, `GET http://up.fixtures.test:${port}/p?q=1 HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: up.fixtures.test\r\nProxy-Connection: keep-alive\r\n\r\n`);
    expect(seen).toMatch(/^GET \/p\?q=1 HTTP\/1\.1\r\n/);
    expect(seen).toMatch(/\r\nConnection: close\r\n/i);
    expect(seen).not.toMatch(/Proxy-Connection/i);
  });

  it("refuses private answers with 403 and reports them", async () => {
    const { proxy, notices } = proxyWith({ "meta.example": ["169.254.169.254"] });
    const proxyPort = await listen(proxy);
    expect(await ask(proxyPort, "CONNECT meta.example:443 HTTP/1.1\r\n\r\n")).toMatch(/^HTTP\/1\.1 403/);
    expect(notices).toEqual([{ origin: "https://meta.example", site: "meta.example", decision: "private_blocked" }]);
  });

  it("refuses out-of-scope sites after taint and reports a denial", async () => {
    const store = new EgressStateStore();
    store.apply({ allowedOrigins: ["https://ok.example"], tainted: true });
    const { proxy, notices } = proxyWith({ "evil.example": ["93.184.216.34"] }, store);
    const proxyPort = await listen(proxy);
    expect(await ask(proxyPort, "CONNECT evil.example:443 HTTP/1.1\r\n\r\n")).toMatch(/^HTTP\/1\.1 403/);
    expect(notices).toEqual([{ origin: "https://evil.example", site: "evil.example", decision: "denied" }]);
  });

  it("answers 400 to malformed or oversized heads and to origin-form requests", async () => {
    const { proxy } = proxyWith({});
    const proxyPort = await listen(proxy);
    expect(await ask(proxyPort, "GET / HTTP/1.1\r\n\r\n")).toMatch(/^HTTP\/1\.1 400/);
    expect(await ask(proxyPort, `CONNECT a:443 HTTP/1.1\r\nX: ${"a".repeat(17_000)}\r\n\r\n`)).toMatch(/^HTTP\/1\.1 400/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the state store and the proxy.** `apps/desktop-host/src/egress/state.ts`:

```ts
import { siteOf, type EgressDecision, type EgressScope, type EgressTarget } from "./decide.ts";

export interface EgressNotice {
  origin: string;
  site: string;
  decision: "allowed_first" | "denied" | "private_blocked";
}

/** The proxy's per-lease state (spec §8.3). Reset only by the VM recycle, which restarts nothing here. */
export class EgressStateStore {
  #allowed: readonly string[] = [];
  #tainted = false;
  readonly #learned = new Set<string>();
  readonly #reported = new Set<string>();

  scope(): EgressScope {
    return { allowedOrigins: this.#allowed, tainted: this.#tainted, learned: this.#learned };
  }

  /** Taint is one-way: a later `tainted: false` is ignored. */
  apply(update: { allowedOrigins: readonly string[]; tainted: boolean }): void {
    this.#allowed = [...update.allowedOrigins];
    if (update.tainted) this.#tainted = true;
  }

  /** A new lease: forget everything (called on recycle). */
  reset(): void {
    this.#allowed = [];
    this.#tainted = false;
    this.#learned.clear();
    this.#reported.clear();
  }

  record(target: EgressTarget, decision: EgressDecision): EgressNotice | null {
    const site = siteOf(target.host);
    if (decision !== "allowed") return { origin: target.origin, site, decision };
    if (!this.#tainted) this.#learned.add(site);
    if (this.#reported.has(site)) return null;
    this.#reported.add(site);
    return { origin: target.origin, site, decision: "allowed_first" };
  }
}
```

`apps/desktop-host/src/egress/proxy.ts`:

```ts
import { connect, createServer, type Server, type Socket } from "node:net";
import type { HostResolver } from "@mastertutor/net-policy";
import { checkedAddress, parseAbsoluteTarget, parseConnectTarget, scopeAllows, type EgressTarget } from "./decide.ts";
import type { EgressNotice, EgressStateStore } from "./state.ts";

export interface ProxyOptions {
  state: EgressStateStore;
  resolve: HostResolver;
  /** SLOT_EGRESS_ALLOW_CIDRS: test fixture networks only (validated by the entrypoint). */
  allowCidrs: readonly string[];
  onNotice(notice: EgressNotice): void;
  connectTimeoutMs?: number;
  idleTimeoutMs?: number;
}

const HEAD_MAX = 16_384;
const reply = (socket: Socket, status: string) =>
  socket.end(`HTTP/1.1 ${status}\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: 0\r\n\r\n`);

/** Reads one request head (≤ 16 KiB); resolves the head and any bytes after it, or null. */
function readHead(socket: Socket): Promise<{ head: string; rest: Buffer } | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    let buffered = Buffer.alloc(0);
    const onData = (chunk: Buffer) => {
      buffered = Buffer.concat([buffered, chunk]);
      const end = buffered.indexOf("\r\n\r\n");
      if (end >= 0 && end + 4 <= HEAD_MAX) {
        socket.off("data", onData);
        socket.pause();
        resolve({ head: buffered.subarray(0, end).toString("latin1"), rest: buffered.subarray(end + 4) });
      } else if (buffered.length > HEAD_MAX) {
        socket.off("data", onData);
        resolve(null);
      }
    };
    socket.on("data", onData);
    socket.once("end", () => resolve(null));
  });
}

/** Origin-form head without hop-by-hop proxy headers; Connection: close unless it is an upgrade. */
function originFormHead(lines: string[], method: string, url: URL): string {
  const headers = lines.slice(1).filter((line) => !/^(proxy-[a-z-]+|connection)\s*:/i.test(line));
  const upgrade = headers.some((line) => /^upgrade\s*:/i.test(line));
  const connection = upgrade ? "Connection: Upgrade" : "Connection: close";
  return [`${method} ${url.pathname}${url.search} HTTP/1.1`, ...headers, connection, "", ""].join("\r\n");
}

/** D53c §8.2: the guest's only way out. CONNECT and absolute-form http; nothing else. */
export function createEgressProxy(options: ProxyOptions): Server {
  return createServer({ pauseOnConnect: false }, async (client) => {
    client.setTimeout(options.idleTimeoutMs ?? 120_000, () => client.destroy());
    client.on("error", () => client.destroy());
    const read = await readHead(client);
    if (!read) return reply(client, "400 Bad Request");
    const lines = read.head.split("\r\n");
    const [method, rawTarget, version] = (lines[0] ?? "").split(" ");
    if (!method || !rawTarget || !/^HTTP\/1\.[01]$/.test(version ?? "")) return reply(client, "400 Bad Request");
    const isConnect = method === "CONNECT";
    const target: EgressTarget | null = isConnect ? parseConnectTarget(rawTarget) : parseAbsoluteTarget(rawTarget);
    if (!target) return reply(client, "400 Bad Request");

    const notify = (decision: "allowed" | "denied" | "private_blocked") => {
      const notice = options.state.record(target, decision);
      if (notice) options.onNotice(notice);
    };
    if (!scopeAllows(target, options.state.scope())) {
      notify("denied");
      return reply(client, "403 Forbidden");
    }
    const address = await checkedAddress(target.host, options.resolve, options.allowCidrs);
    if (!address) {
      notify("private_blocked");
      return reply(client, "403 Forbidden");
    }
    notify("allowed");

    const upstream = connect({ host: address, port: target.port });
    upstream.setTimeout(options.connectTimeoutMs ?? 10_000, () => upstream.destroy());
    upstream.once("error", () => (client.writable ? reply(client, "502 Bad Gateway") : client.destroy()));
    upstream.once("connect", () => {
      upstream.setTimeout(options.idleTimeoutMs ?? 120_000, () => upstream.destroy());
      if (isConnect) client.write("HTTP/1.1 200 Connection Established\r\n\r\n");
      else upstream.write(originFormHead(lines, method, new URL(rawTarget)));
      if (read.rest.length > 0) upstream.write(read.rest);
      client.pipe(upstream).pipe(client);
      client.resume();
      upstream.once("close", () => client.destroy());
      client.once("close", () => upstream.destroy());
    });
  });
}
```

```ts
// vmm.ts
export class VmmError extends Error { readonly path: string; readonly status: number }
export interface VmmApi { call(method: "PUT" | "PATCH", path: string, body: unknown): Promise<void> }
export function createVmmApi(socketPath: string, timeoutMs?: number): VmmApi;
export interface VmConfig { kernel: string; rootfs: string; vcpus: number; memMib: number; tap: string; guestMac: string; vsockUds: string }
export interface SnapshotFiles { state: string; mem: string }
```

```ts
import { mkdtemp, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { createServer, type Server } from "node:http";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { coldBoot, createSnapshot, createVmmApi, loadSnapshot, pauseVm, VmmError } from "./vmm.ts";

let dir: string;
let server: Server;
let calls: Array<{ method: string; url: string; body: unknown }>;
let failPath: string | null;

beforeEach(async () => {
  dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "vmm-"));
  calls = [];
  failPath = null;
  server = createServer((req, res) => {
    let text = "";
    req.on("data", (chunk) => (text += chunk));
    req.on("end", () => {
      calls.push({ method: req.method!, url: req.url!, body: JSON.parse(text || "null") });
      if (req.url === failPath) {
        res.writeHead(400, { "content-type": "application/json" }).end(JSON.stringify({ fault_message: "bad" }));
      } else res.writeHead(204).end();
    });
  });
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.listen(join(dir, "fc.sock"), resolve));
});
afterEach(async () => {
  await new Promise((resolve) => server.close(resolve));
  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

const config = {
  kernel: "/opt/vm/vmlinux", rootfs: "/opt/vm/rootfs.ext4", vcpus: 2, memMib: 4096,
  tap: "tap0", guestMac: "06:00:c0:a8:7f:02", vsockUds: "/run/vm/vsock.sock",
};

describe("Firecracker API client", () => {
  it("cold-boots with a read-only root, one tap, one vsock and nothing else", async () => {
    await coldBoot(createVmmApi(join(dir, "fc.sock")), config);
    expect(calls.map((call) => `${call.method} ${call.url}`)).toEqual([
      "PUT /boot-source", "PUT /drives/rootfs", "PUT /machine-config",
      "PUT /network-interfaces/eth0", "PUT /vsock", "PUT /actions",
    ]);
    expect(calls[1]?.body).toMatchObject({ is_read_only: true, is_root_device: true });
    expect(calls[4]?.body).toEqual({ guest_cid: 3, uds_path: "/run/vm/vsock.sock" });
    expect(calls[5]?.body).toEqual({ action_type: "InstanceStart" });
  });

  it("pauses, snapshots fully, and restores from a MAP_PRIVATE file backend", async () => {
    const api = createVmmApi(join(dir, "fc.sock"));
    const files = { state: "/run/vm/golden.vmstate", mem: "/run/vm/golden.mem" };
    await pauseVm(api);

    await loadSnapshot(api, files);

      { method: "PUT", url: "/snapshot/create", body: { snapshot_type: "Full", snapshot_path: files.state, mem_file_path: files.mem } },
      { method: "PUT", url: "/snapshot/load", body: { snapshot_path: files.state, mem_backend: { backend_type: "File", backend_path: files.mem }, resume_vm: true } },

  it("surfaces Firecracker's fault as a VmmError with the path", async () => {
    failPath = "/snapshot/load";

    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(VmmError);
    expect(error).toMatchObject({ path: "/snapshot/load", status: 400 });
  });
});
```

`apps/desktop-host/src/vsock.test.ts` fakes Firecracker's host-side vsock multiplexer:

```ts
import { mkdtemp, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { createServer, connect, type Server } from "node:net";
import { once } from "node:events";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { dialGuest, listenForGuest } from "./vsock.ts";

let dir: string;
const servers: Server[] = [];
beforeEach(async () => void (dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "vsock-"))));
afterEach(async () => {
  for (const server of servers.splice(0)) server.close();
  await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
});

describe("vsock", () => {
  it("dials a guest port with the CONNECT handshake and returns the stream after OK", async () => {
    const uds = join(dir, "v.sock");
    const muxer = createServer((socket) =>
      socket.once("data", (chunk) => {
        expect(chunk.toString()).toBe("CONNECT 51\n");
        socket.write("OK 1073741824\nfrom-guest");
      }),
    );
    servers.push(muxer);
    muxer.listen(uds);
    await once(muxer, "listening");
    const stream = await dialGuest(uds, 51);
    const [data] = await once(stream, "data");
    expect(String(data)).toBe("from-guest");
    stream.destroy();
  });

  it("fails when the guest port refuses", async () => {
    const uds = join(dir, "v.sock");
    const muxer = createServer((socket) => socket.end());
    servers.push(muxer);
    muxer.listen(uds);
    await once(muxer, "listening");
    await expect(dialGuest(uds, 52, 500)).rejects.toThrow(/vsock/);
  });

  it("accepts guest-initiated lines on <uds>_<port>, each at most 64 bytes", async () => {
    const uds = join(dir, "v.sock");
    const lines: string[] = [];
    servers.push(await listenForGuest(uds, 1025, (line) => lines.push(line)));
    const guest = connect(`${uds}_1025`);
    guest.end(`golden\n${"x".repeat(100)}\n`);
    await once(guest, "close");
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 20));
    expect(lines).toEqual(["golden"]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `apps/desktop-host/src/vmm.ts`:

```ts
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { watch } from "node:fs";
import { access, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { basename, dirname } from "node:path";
import { request } from "node:http";

export class VmmError extends Error {
  readonly path: string;
  readonly status: number;
  constructor(path: string, status: number, message: string) {
    super(`firecracker ${path}: ${status} ${message.slice(0, 200)}`);
    this.name = "VmmError";
    this.path = path;
    this.status = status;
  }
}

export interface VmmApi {
  call(method: "PUT" | "PATCH", path: string, body: unknown): Promise<void>;
}

/** Firecracker's REST API over its unix socket; 2xx is success, anything else a VmmError. */
export function createVmmApi(socketPath: string, timeoutMs = 5_000): VmmApi {
  return {
    call: (method, path, body) =>
      new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const payload = JSON.stringify(body);
        const req = request(
          {
            socketPath,
            method,
            path,
            timeout: timeoutMs,
            headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "content-length": Buffer.byteLength(payload) },
          },
          (res) => {
            let text = "";
            res.setEncoding("utf8");
            res.on("data", (chunk: string) => {
              if (text.length < 4_096) text += chunk;
            });
            res.on("end", () => {
              const status = res.statusCode ?? 0;
              if (status >= 200 && status < 300) return resolve();
              let fault = "";
              try {
                fault = String((JSON.parse(text) as { fault_message?: unknown }).fault_message ?? "");
              } catch {
                fault = "";
              }
              reject(new VmmError(path, status, fault));
            });
          },
        );
        req.on("timeout", () => req.destroy(new VmmError(path, 0, "timeout")));
        req.on("error", reject);
        req.end(payload);
      }),
  };
}

export interface VmConfig {
  kernel: string;
  rootfs: string;
  vcpus: number;
  memMib: number;
  tap: string;
  guestMac: string;
  vsockUds: string;
}
export interface SnapshotFiles {
  state: string;
  mem: string;
}

export const BOOT_ARGS = "console=ttyS0 reboot=k panic=1 pci=off ro init=/sbin/vm-init";

/** Spec §5.4: a read-only root, one tap, one vsock; no other device. */
export async function coldBoot(api: VmmApi, config: VmConfig): Promise<void> {
  await api.call("PUT", "/boot-source", { kernel_image_path: config.kernel, boot_args: BOOT_ARGS });
  await api.call("PUT", "/drives/rootfs", {
    drive_id: "rootfs",
    path_on_host: config.rootfs,
    is_root_device: true,
    is_read_only: true,
  });
  await api.call("PUT", "/machine-config", { vcpu_count: config.vcpus, mem_size_mib: config.memMib, smt: false });
  await api.call("PUT", "/network-interfaces/eth0", {
    iface_id: "eth0",
    host_dev_name: config.tap,
    guest_mac: config.guestMac,
  });
  await api.call("PUT", "/vsock", { guest_cid: 3, uds_path: config.vsockUds });
  await api.call("PUT", "/actions", { action_type: "InstanceStart" });
}

export const pauseVm = (api: VmmApi) => api.call("PATCH", "/vm", { state: "Paused" });
export const createSnapshot = (api: VmmApi, files: SnapshotFiles) =>
  api.call("PUT", "/snapshot/create", { snapshot_type: "Full", snapshot_path: files.state, mem_file_path: files.mem });
/** The memory file is mapped MAP_PRIVATE: the golden snapshot is never written (spec §5.4). */
export const loadSnapshot = (api: VmmApi, files: SnapshotFiles) =>
  api.call("PUT", "/snapshot/load", {
    snapshot_path: files.state,
    mem_backend: { backend_type: "File", backend_path: files.mem },
    resume_vm: true,
  });

export interface VmmProcess {
  api: VmmApi;
  /** Resolves when the VMM process exits, for any reason. */
  exited: Promise<void>;
  /** SIGKILL, then waits for the exit: a guest cannot delay it. */
  kill(): Promise<void>;
}

/** Spawns firecracker and waits (by fs.watch, not polling) for its API socket to appear. */
export async function launchVmm(options: {
  binary: string;
  socketPath: string;
  startTimeoutMs?: number;
}): Promise<VmmProcess> {
  await rm(options.socketPath, { force: true });
  const child = spawn(options.binary, ["--api-sock", options.socketPath, "--level", "Warn"], {
    stdio: ["ignore", "inherit", "inherit"],
  });
  const exited = new Promise<void>((resolve) => child.once("exit", () => resolve()));
  const appeared = new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const watcher = watch(dirname(options.socketPath), (_event, name) => {
      if (name === basename(options.socketPath)) {
        watcher.close();
        resolve();
      }
    });
    access(options.socketPath).then(() => {
      watcher.close();
      resolve();
    }, () => undefined);
    setTimeout(() => {
      watcher.close();
      reject(new Error("firecracker API socket did not appear"));
    }, options.startTimeoutMs ?? 2_000).unref();
    void exited.then(() => reject(new Error("firecracker exited during start")));
  });
  try {
    await appeared;
  } catch (error) {
    child.kill("SIGKILL");
    throw error;
  }
  return {
    api: createVmmApi(options.socketPath),
    exited,
    async kill() {
      child.kill("SIGKILL");
      await exited;
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/desktop-host/src/vsock.ts`:

```ts
import { connect, createServer, type Server, type Socket } from "node:net";
import { rm } from "node:fs/promises";

/** Firecracker host-initiated vsock: connect to the UDS, send "CONNECT <port>\n", expect "OK <n>\n". */
export function dialGuest(udsPath: string, port: number, timeoutMs = 2_000): Promise<Socket> {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const socket = connect(udsPath);
    let reply = "";
    const fail = (why: string) => {
      socket.destroy();
      reject(new Error(`vsock ${port}: ${why}`));
    };
    const timer = setTimeout(() => fail("handshake timeout"), timeoutMs);
    socket.once("connect", () => socket.write(`CONNECT ${port}\n`));
    const onData = (chunk: Buffer) => {
      reply += chunk.toString("latin1");
      const end = reply.indexOf("\n");
      if (end < 0) {
        if (reply.length > 64) fail("bad handshake");
        return;
      }
      socket.off("data", onData);
      clearTimeout(timer);
      if (!/^OK [0-9]+$/.test(reply.slice(0, end))) return fail("refused");
      const rest = Buffer.from(reply.slice(end + 1), "latin1");
      if (rest.length > 0) socket.unshift(rest);
      resolve(socket);
    };
    socket.on("data", onData);
    socket.once("error", (error) => fail(error.message));
    socket.once("close", () => fail("closed"));
  });
}

/** Guest-initiated vsock to host port P arrives on "<uds>_<P>". Lines over 64 bytes are dropped. */
export async function listenForGuest(
  udsPath: string,
  port: number,
  onLine: (line: string) => void,
): Promise<Server> {
  const path = `${udsPath}_${port}`;
  await rm(path, { force: true });
  const server = createServer((socket) => {
    let buffered = "";
    socket.setEncoding("latin1");
    socket.on("data", (chunk: string) => {
      buffered += chunk;
      for (let end = buffered.indexOf("\n"); end >= 0; end = buffered.indexOf("\n")) {
        const line = buffered.slice(0, end);
        buffered = buffered.slice(end + 1);
        if (line.length <= 64) onLine(line);
      }
      if (buffered.length > 64) socket.destroy();
    });
    socket.on("error", () => socket.destroy());
  });
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.listen(path, resolve));
  return server;
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { VmController, type ControllerDeps } from "./controller.ts";
import type { VmmApi, VmmProcess } from "./vmm.ts";

function fakeDeps(over: Partial<ControllerDeps> = {}) {
  const log: string[] = [];
  const processes: Array<{ exit(): void; killed: boolean }> = [];
  let ids = 0;
  const launch = async (): Promise<VmmProcess> => {
    let exit!: () => void;
    const exited = new Promise<void>((resolve) => (exit = resolve));
    const entry = { exit, killed: false };
    processes.push(entry);
    const api: VmmApi = { call: async (method, path) => void log.push(`${method} ${path}`) };
    return { api, exited, kill: async () => { entry.killed = true; exit(); log.push("kill"); } };
  };
  const deps: ControllerDeps = {
    launch,
    config: { kernel: "k", rootfs: "r", vcpus: 2, memMib: 4096, tap: "tap0", guestMac: "06:00:00:00:00:02", vsockUds: "/run/vm/vsock.sock" },
    files: { state: "/run/vm/golden.vmstate", mem: "/run/vm/golden.mem" },
    waitGolden: async () => void log.push("golden"),
    sendStart: async () => void log.push("start"),
    now: () => 1_000,
    newVmId: () => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-00000000000${++ids}`,
    log: { info: () => undefined, error: () => undefined },
    ...over,
  };
  return { deps, log, processes };
}

describe("VmController", () => {
  it("takes the golden snapshot while paused, then kills that VMM", async () => {
    const { deps, log } = fakeDeps();
    await new VmController(deps).prepareGolden();
    expect(log).toEqual([
      "PUT /boot-source", "PUT /drives/rootfs", "PUT /machine-config", "PUT /network-interfaces/eth0",
      "PUT /vsock", "PUT /actions", "golden", "PATCH /vm", "PUT /snapshot/create", "kill",
    ]);
  });

  it("recycles by SIGKILL and restore, then sends start, with a fresh vmId each time", async () => {
    const { deps, log } = fakeDeps();
    const controller = new VmController(deps);
    await controller.prepareGolden();
    log.length = 0;
    const first = await controller.recycle();
    const second = await controller.recycle();
    expect(log).toEqual(["PUT /snapshot/load", "start", "kill", "PUT /snapshot/load", "start"]);
    expect(first.vmId).not.toBe(second.vmId);
    expect(controller.vmId).toBe(second.vmId);
  });

  it("refuses to recycle before the golden snapshot exists", async () => {
    const { deps } = fakeDeps();
    await expect(new VmController(deps).recycle()).rejects.toThrow(/golden/);
  });

  it("serialises concurrent recycles", async () => {
    const { deps, log } = fakeDeps();
    const controller = new VmController(deps);
    await controller.prepareGolden();
    log.length = 0;
    await Promise.all([controller.recycle(), controller.recycle()]);
    expect(log).toEqual(["PUT /snapshot/load", "start", "kill", "PUT /snapshot/load", "start"]);
  });

  it("restores a VM that exited on its own (Chromium closed, guest halted) and changes vmId", async () => {
    const { deps, processes } = fakeDeps();
    const controller = new VmController(deps);
    await controller.prepareGolden();
    const { vmId } = await controller.recycle();
    processes.at(-1)!.exit(); // the guest halted
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10));
    expect(controller.vmId).not.toBe(vmId);
    expect(processes.length).toBe(3); // golden, first restore, automatic restore
  });

  it("clears vmId while a recycle is in flight, so no stale id ever reads as ready", async () => {
    let release!: () => void;
    const { deps } = fakeDeps({ sendStart: () => new Promise<void>((resolve) => (release = resolve)) });
    const controller = new VmController(deps);
    await controller.prepareGolden();
    const pending = controller.recycle();
    await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 0));
    expect(controller.vmId).toBeNull();
    release();
    await pending;
    expect(controller.vmId).not.toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `apps/desktop-host/src/controller.ts`:

```ts
import type { RecycleResult } from "@mastertutor/contracts";
import { coldBoot, createSnapshot, loadSnapshot, pauseVm, type SnapshotFiles, type VmConfig, type VmmProcess } from "./vmm.ts";

export interface ControllerDeps {
  launch(): Promise<VmmProcess>;
  config: VmConfig;
  files: SnapshotFiles;
  /** Resolves when vm-init writes "golden" to host vsock 1025. */
  waitGolden(timeoutMs: number): Promise<void>;
  /** Dials guest vsock 51 and sends "start <epoch_ms>". */
  sendStart(epochMs: number): Promise<void>;
  /** A new lease begins: per-lease state elsewhere (the proxy's) is forgotten. */
  onRecycled?(): void;
  now(): number;
  newVmId(): string;
  log: { info(fields: object, message: string): void; error(fields: object, message: string): void };
}

/**
 * Spec §5.4. Owns the one VMM of this container. A recycle always SIGKILLs and restores from the
 * golden snapshot (MAP_PRIVATE), so nothing a run did survives and a guest cannot refuse it. Every
 * restore gets a new vmId: the agent's readiness id, which therefore changes only on a host-side
 * restore, never because a guest restarted Chromium (SlotPool's no-leftover rule).
 */
export class VmController {
  readonly #deps: ControllerDeps;
  #vmm: VmmProcess | null = null;
  #vmId: string | null = null;
  #golden = false;
  #queue: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve();
  #deliberate = new WeakSet<VmmProcess>();

  constructor(deps: ControllerDeps) {
    this.#deps = deps;
  }

  get vmId(): string | null {
    return this.#vmId;
  }

  async prepareGolden(timeoutMs = 60_000): Promise<void> {
    const vmm = await this.#deps.launch();
    this.#deliberate.add(vmm);
    try {
      await coldBoot(vmm.api, this.#deps.config);
      await this.#deps.waitGolden(timeoutMs);
      await pauseVm(vmm.api);
      await createSnapshot(vmm.api, this.#deps.files);
      this.#golden = true;
    } finally {
      await vmm.kill();
    }
  }

  recycle(): Promise<RecycleResult> {
    const work = this.#queue.then(() => this.#restore());
    this.#queue = work.catch(() => undefined);
    return work;
  }

  async #restore(): Promise<RecycleResult> {
    if (!this.#golden) throw new Error("no golden snapshot yet");
    this.#vmId = null;
    const started = this.#deps.now();
    if (this.#vmm) {
      this.#deliberate.add(this.#vmm);
      await this.#vmm.kill();
      this.#vmm = null;
    }
    this.#deps.onRecycled?.();
    const vmm = await this.#deps.launch();
    this.#vmm = vmm;
    void vmm.exited.then(() => this.#onExit(vmm));
    await loadSnapshot(vmm.api, this.#deps.files);
    const restoreMs = Math.max(0, Math.round(this.#deps.now() - started));
    await this.#deps.sendStart(Date.now());
    const vmId = this.#deps.newVmId();
    this.#vmId = vmId;
    this.#deps.log.info({ restoreMs }, "vm restored");
    return { vmId, restoreMs };
  }

  /** A VM that exits by itself (Chromium closed → the guest halts) is restored at once. */
  #onExit(vmm: VmmProcess): void {
    if (this.#deliberate.has(vmm) || this.#vmm !== vmm) return;
    this.#vmm = null;
    this.#vmId = null;
    this.#deps.log.error({}, "vm exited on its own; restoring");
    this.recycle().catch((error: unknown) =>
      this.#deps.log.error({ err: String(error) }, "automatic restore failed"),
    );
  }
}
```

```ts
import { once } from "node:events";
import type { AddressInfo } from "node:net";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createControlServer } from "./control-server.ts";

async function serve(controller: Parameters<typeof createControlServer>[0]) {
  const server = createControlServer(controller);
  server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1");
  await once(server, "listening");
  return { server, url: `http://127.0.0.1:${(server.address() as AddressInfo).port}` };
}

describe("control server", () => {
  const vmId = "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";

  it("recycles on POST /recycle and reports the vm on GET /vm", async () => {
    const { server, url } = await serve({ vmId, recycle: async () => ({ vmId, restoreMs: 140 }) });
    expect(await (await fetch(`${url}/recycle`, { method: "POST" })).json()).toEqual({ vmId, restoreMs: 140 });
    expect(await (await fetch(`${url}/vm`)).json()).toEqual({ vmId });
    server.close();
  });

  it("answers 500 without detail when the recycle fails, and 404 elsewhere", async () => {
    const { server, url } = await serve({ vmId: null, recycle: async () => { throw new Error("secret detail"); } });
    const failed = await fetch(`${url}/recycle`, { method: "POST" });
    expect(failed.status).toBe(500);
    expect(await failed.text()).not.toMatch(/secret detail/);
    expect((await fetch(`${url}/nope`)).status).toBe(404);
    server.close();
  });
});
```

`apps/desktop-host/src/relay.test.ts`:

```ts
import { once } from "node:events";
import { connect, createServer, type AddressInfo, type Socket } from "node:net";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createDeskRelay } from "./relay.ts";

describe("deskd relay", () => {
  it("pipes each agent connection to a fresh guest stream", async () => {
    const guest = createServer((socket) => socket.pipe(socket)); // echo
    guest.listen(0, "127.0.0.1");
    await once(guest, "listening");
    const dial = () => new Promise<Socket>((resolve) => {
      const socket = connect((guest.address() as AddressInfo).port, "127.0.0.1", () => resolve(socket));
    });
    const relay = createDeskRelay(dial);
    relay.listen(0, "127.0.0.1");
    await once(relay, "listening");
    const agent = connect((relay.address() as AddressInfo).port, "127.0.0.1");
    agent.write("ping");
    const [data] = await once(agent, "data");
    expect(String(data)).toBe("ping");
    agent.destroy();
    relay.close();
    guest.close();
  });

  it("closes the agent side when the guest cannot be reached", async () => {
    const relay = createDeskRelay(async () => {
      throw new Error("vsock 52: refused");
    });
    relay.listen(0, "127.0.0.1");
    await once(relay, "listening");
    const agent = connect((relay.address() as AddressInfo).port, "127.0.0.1");
    await once(agent, "close");
    relay.close();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `apps/desktop-host/src/control-server.ts`:

```ts
import { createServer, type Server, type ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import { RecycleResult, VmState } from "@mastertutor/contracts";
import type { VmController } from "./controller.ts";

const send = (res: ServerResponse, status: number, body: unknown) =>
  res.writeHead(status, { "content-type": "application/json" }).end(JSON.stringify(body));

/** Spec §8.4: the agent-only control API (nftables admits only CDP_ALLOWED_IP on this port). */
export function createControlServer(controller: Pick<VmController, "recycle" | "vmId">): Server {
  return createServer(async (req, res) => {
    try {
      if (req.method === "POST" && req.url === "/recycle")
        return send(res, 200, RecycleResult.parse(await controller.recycle()));
      if (req.method === "GET" && req.url === "/vm")
        return send(res, 200, VmState.parse({ vmId: controller.vmId }));
      send(res, 404, { error: "not_found" });
    } catch {
      send(res, 500, { error: "failed" });
    }
  });
}
```

`apps/desktop-host/src/relay.ts`:

```ts
import { createServer, type Server, type Socket } from "node:net";

/** DESK_PORT → guest vsock 52 (spec §7.2). One guest stream per agent connection. */
export function createDeskRelay(dial: () => Promise<Socket>, maxConnections = 4): Server {
  const server = createServer(async (agent) => {
    agent.pause();
    agent.on("error", () => agent.destroy());
    let guest: Socket;
    try {
      guest = await dial();
    } catch {
      agent.destroy();
      return;
    }
    guest.on("error", () => guest.destroy());
    agent.pipe(guest).pipe(agent);
    agent.resume();
    agent.once("close", () => guest.destroy());
    guest.once("close", () => agent.destroy());
  });
  server.maxConnections = maxConnections;
  return server;
}
```

`apps/desktop-host/src/main.ts` (the controller process, run as uid fc):

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { DESK_PORT, DESKTOP_CONTROL_PORT } from "@mastertutor/contracts";
import { z } from "zod";
import { createControlServer } from "./control-server.ts";
import { VmController } from "./controller.ts";
import { createDeskRelay } from "./relay.ts";
import { launchVmm } from "./vmm.ts";
import { dialGuest, listenForGuest } from "./vsock.ts";

const env = z
  .object({
    DESKTOP_VM_VCPUS: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(8).default(2),
    DESKTOP_VM_MEM_MIB: z.coerce.number().int().min(1024).max(16_384).default(4096),
  })
  .parse(process.env);

const VSOCK = "/run/vm/vsock.sock";
const line = (fields: object, message: string, level: string) =>
  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ level, msg: message, service: "desktop-host", ...fields })}\n`);
const log = { info: (f: object, m: string) => line(f, m, "info"), error: (f: object, m: string) => line(f, m, "error") };

let golden: (() => void) | null = null;
await listenForGuest(VSOCK, 1025, (text) => {
  if (text === "golden") golden?.();
});

const controller = new VmController({
  launch: () => launchVmm({ binary: "/usr/local/bin/firecracker", socketPath: "/run/vm/fc.sock" }),
  config: {
    kernel: "/opt/vm/vmlinux",
    rootfs: "/opt/vm/rootfs.ext4",
    vcpus: env.DESKTOP_VM_VCPUS,
    memMib: env.DESKTOP_VM_MEM_MIB,
    tap: "tap0",
    guestMac: "06:00:c0:a8:7f:02",
    vsockUds: VSOCK,
  },
  files: { state: "/run/vm/golden.vmstate", mem: "/run/vm/golden.mem" },
  waitGolden: (timeoutMs) =>
    new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      golden = resolve;
      setTimeout(() => reject(new Error("guest never reached golden")), timeoutMs).unref();
    }),
  sendStart: async (epochMs) => {
    const socket = await dialGuest(VSOCK, 51);
    socket.end(`start ${epochMs}\n`);
  },
  now: () => performance.now(),
  newVmId: randomUUID,
  log,
});

await controller.prepareGolden();
await controller.recycle(); // a never-leased slot is ready as soon as its browser answers
createControlServer(controller).listen(DESKTOP_CONTROL_PORT, "0.0.0.0");
createDeskRelay(() => dialGuest(VSOCK, 52)).listen(DESK_PORT, "0.0.0.0");
log.info({}, "desktop host ready");
```

`apps/desktop-host/src/egress/main.ts` (the proxy process, run as uid proxy):

```ts
import { dnsResolver } from "@mastertutor/net-policy";
import { createEgressProxy } from "./proxy.ts";
import { EgressStateStore } from "./state.ts";

const allowCidrs = (process.env.SLOT_EGRESS_ALLOW_CIDRS ?? "").split(",").map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
const state = new EgressStateStore();
const proxy = createEgressProxy({
  state,
  resolve: dnsResolver,
  allowCidrs,
  // Registrable domain only: never a path, query or full URL (V12).
  onNotice: (notice) =>
    process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify({ level: "info", service: "egress-proxy", site: notice.site, decision: notice.decision })}\n`),
});
proxy.listen(3128, "192.168.127.1");
```

## Task P1.12: The `guest` stage: `vm-init`, guest programs, `deskd` (`hello`, `fileGet`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/browser-slot/Dockerfile`. Name the existing final stage `slot-image` (`FROM ghcr.io/m1k1o/neko/chromium:3.1.6@sha256:… AS slot-image`). Add the `guest` stage after it, and end the file with `FROM slot-image AS slot`, so the default target and every existing `docker build apps/browser-slot` caller still produce the browser slot unchanged.
- Create: `apps/browser-slot/guest/vm-init`
