**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the `DESK_METHODS` P1 subset, `DESK_LIMITS` and `DESK_HOME` (Task P1.6), mirrored in Python.
- Produces:
  - `/sbin/vm-init` (guest PID 1);
  - vsock protocol: the guest writes `golden\n` to host port 1025, and reads `start <13-digit epoch ms> <base64url JSON env>\n` on guest port 51. The env JSON keys are `NEKO_MEMBER_PROVIDER`, `NEKO_MEMBER_OBJECT_USERS`, `NEKO_WEBRTC_NAT1TO1`, `NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX`, `NEKO_WEBRTC_TCPMUX` and `PULSE_ALLOWED_IP`;
  - `deskd` on vsock port 52.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing Python tests.** `apps/browser-slot/guest/deskd/test_deskd.py`:

```python
import io
import json
import os
import tempfile
import unittest

import deskd


def frame(header, payload=b""):
    return json.dumps(header).encode() + b"\n" + payload


class PathRule(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_only_below_home_after_realpath(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as home:
            deskd.HOME = home + "/"
            inside = os.path.join(home, "a.txt")
            open(inside, "w").close()
            os.symlink("/etc/passwd", os.path.join(home, "link"))
            self.assertEqual(deskd.safe_path(inside), inside)
            for bad in ("/etc/passwd", os.path.join(home, "link"), home, home + "/../x", "a\0b", "x" * 600):
                with self.assertRaises(deskd.DeskError, msg=bad):
                    deskd.safe_path(bad)


class Protocol(unittest.TestCase):
    def serve(self, data):
        out = io.BytesIO()
        deskd.serve(io.BufferedReader(io.BytesIO(data)), out)
        lines = []
        raw = out.getvalue()
        while raw:
            head, raw = raw.split(b"\n", 1)
            header = json.loads(head)
            payload, raw = raw[: header.get("bytes", 0)], raw[header.get("bytes", 0):]
            lines.append((header, payload))
        return lines

    def test_hello_lists_exactly_the_methods(self):
        [(header, _)] = self.serve(frame({"id": 1, "method": "hello", "params": {}, "bytes": 0}))
        self.assertEqual(header, {"id": 1, "ok": True, "result": {"methods": ["hello", "fileGet"], "version": "1"}, "bytes": 0})

    def test_file_get_streams_bytes_and_enforces_max(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as home:
            deskd.HOME = home + "/"
            path = os.path.join(home, "f.bin")
            with open(path, "wb") as f:
                f.write(b"abc")
            [(ok, payload)] = self.serve(frame({"id": 2, "method": "fileGet", "params": {"path": path, "maxBytes": 10}, "bytes": 0}))
            self.assertEqual((ok["ok"], ok["result"], payload), (True, {"size": 3}, b"abc"))
            [(err, _)] = self.serve(frame({"id": 3, "method": "fileGet", "params": {"path": path, "maxBytes": 2}, "bytes": 0}))
            self.assertEqual(err["error"]["code"], "too_large")

    def test_unknown_methods_and_bad_headers_answer_errors(self):
        [(unknown, _)] = self.serve(frame({"id": 4, "method": "exec", "params": {}, "bytes": 0}))
        self.assertEqual(unknown["error"]["code"], "unknown_method")
        self.assertEqual(self.serve(b"{not json\n")[0][0]["error"]["code"], "bad_header")
        self.assertEqual(self.serve(b"x" * 20000 + b"\n")[0][0]["error"]["code"], "bad_header")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `apps/browser-slot/guest/deskd/deskd.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""deskd (D53c spec §7): the agent's only helper inside the desktop guest, on vsock port 52.

Wire: one JSON header line, then exactly header["bytes"] raw bytes, each way. The method list and
limits mirror packages/contracts/src/desk.ts (a behaviour test compares them through `hello`).
Runs as the desktop user, never root.
"""
import json
import os
import socket
import stat
import sys

METHODS = ("hello", "fileGet")
VERSION = "1"
HOME = "/home/neko/"
HEADER_MAX = 16384
PAYLOAD_MAX = 64 * 1024 * 1024
PATH_MAX = 512


class DeskError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code, message):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.message = message[:200]


def safe_path(path):
    if not isinstance(path, str) or not path or len(path) > PATH_MAX or "\0" in path:
        raise DeskError("bad_path", "invalid path")
    real = os.path.realpath(path)
    if not real.startswith(HOME) or len(real) <= len(HOME):
        raise DeskError("bad_path", "path is outside the home folder")
    return real


def _limit(params, key):
    value = params.get(key)
    if not isinstance(value, int) or isinstance(value, bool) or value < 1 or value > PAYLOAD_MAX:
        raise DeskError("bad_params", "invalid " + key)
    return value


def hello(_params, _payload):
    return {"methods": list(METHODS), "version": VERSION}, b""


def file_get(params, _payload):
    path = safe_path(params.get("path"))
    limit = _limit(params, "maxBytes")
    try:
        info = os.stat(path)
    except OSError:
        raise DeskError("not_found", "no such file")
    if not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode):
        raise DeskError("not_a_file", "not a regular file")
    with open(path, "rb") as handle:
        data = handle.read(limit + 1)
    if len(data) > limit:
        raise DeskError("too_large", "file is larger than maxBytes")
    return {"size": len(data)}, data


HANDLERS = {"hello": hello, "fileGet": file_get}


def _answer(out, header, payload=b""):
    out.write(json.dumps(header, separators=(",", ":")).encode() + b"\n" + payload)
    out.flush()


def _read_exact(stream, size):
    data = stream.read(size) if size else b""
    if len(data) != size:
        raise DeskError("bad_payload", "short payload")
    return data


def serve(stream, out):
    """Answers requests until the stream ends or a header is malformed (then answers and stops)."""
    while True:
        line = stream.readline(HEADER_MAX + 1)
        if not line:
            return
        request_id = 0
        try:
            if len(line) > HEADER_MAX or not line.endswith(b"\n"):
                raise DeskError("bad_header", "header too long")
            try:
                header = json.loads(line)
            except ValueError:
                raise DeskError("bad_header", "header is not JSON")
            if not isinstance(header, dict):
                raise DeskError("bad_header", "header is not an object")
            request_id = header.get("id") if isinstance(header.get("id"), int) else 0
            size = header.get("bytes", 0)
            if not isinstance(size, int) or size < 0 or size > PAYLOAD_MAX:
                raise DeskError("bad_header", "invalid bytes")
            payload = _read_exact(stream, size)
            handler = HANDLERS.get(header.get("method"))
            if handler is None:
                raise DeskError("unknown_method", "unknown method")
            params = header.get("params")
            result, data = handler(params if isinstance(params, dict) else {}, payload)
            _answer(out, {"id": request_id, "ok": True, "result": result, "bytes": len(data)}, data)
        except DeskError as error:
            _answer(out, {"id": max(request_id, 1), "ok": False,
                          "error": {"code": error.code, "message": error.message}, "bytes": 0})
            if error.code in ("bad_header", "bad_payload"):
                return


def main():
    listener = socket.socket(socket.AF_VSOCK, socket.SOCK_STREAM)
    listener.bind((socket.VMADDR_CID_ANY, 52))
    listener.listen(4)
    while True:
        connection, _ = listener.accept()
        if os.fork() == 0:
            listener.close()
            with connection, connection.makefile("rb") as stream, connection.makefile("wb") as out:
                serve(stream, out)
            os._exit(0)
        connection.close()
        while True:
            try:
                if os.waitpid(-1, os.WNOHANG) == (0, 0):
                    break
            except ChildProcessError:
                break


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `vm-init`.** `apps/browser-slot/guest/vm-init` (mode 0755). The Xorg and openbox command lines are the ones the `slot-image` stage's n.eko supervisord programs use, as recorded in the P0 report.

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Guest PID 1 (D53c spec §6.2).
#   stage 1: a RAM overlay over the read-only root, then pivot into it;
#   stage 2: mounts, network, X and the window manager, report "golden" (the snapshot is taken
#            here), then wait for the host's "start" line and run the session under supervisord.
set -eu
if [ "${1:-}" != stage2 ]; then
  mount -t tmpfs -o size=1536m,mode=0755 rw /mnt/rw
  mkdir -p /mnt/rw/upper /mnt/rw/work /mnt/rw/root
  mount -t overlay overlay -o lowerdir=/,upperdir=/mnt/rw/upper,workdir=/mnt/rw/work /mnt/rw/root
  mkdir -p /mnt/rw/root/.oldroot
  cd /mnt/rw/root
  pivot_root . .oldroot
  exec chroot . /sbin/vm-init stage2
fi
umount -l /.oldroot
mount -t proc proc /proc
mount -t sysfs sysfs /sys
mount -t devtmpfs devtmpfs /dev
mkdir -p /dev/pts /dev/shm
mount -t devpts devpts /dev/pts
mount -t tmpfs -o size=1g,mode=1777 shm /dev/shm
mount -t tmpfs -o mode=0755 run /run
mkdir -p /var/log/neko /var/log/guest /tmp/chromium-profile
chown neko:neko /var/log/neko /tmp/chromium-profile
chmod 700 /tmp/chromium-profile
ip link set lo up
ip addr add 192.168.127.2/30 dev eth0
ip link set eth0 up
ip route add default via 192.168.127.1
export DISPLAY=:99.0
/usr/bin/X -config /etc/neko/xorg.conf -noreset -nolisten tcp "$DISPLAY" >/var/log/neko/xorg.log 2>&1 &
while [ ! -S /tmp/.X11-unix/X99 ]; do sleep 0.05; done
su neko -s /bin/sh -c "DISPLAY=$DISPLAY openbox --config-file /etc/neko/openbox.xml" >/var/log/neko/openbox.log 2>&1 &
echo golden | socat -u - VSOCK-CONNECT:2:1025
# --- golden snapshot point: everything above is shared by every run; nothing below is ---
line="$(socat -u VSOCK-LISTEN:51 - | head -c 8192 | head -n 1)"
case "$line" in
  "start "[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]" "*) ;;
  *) reboot -f ;;
esac
set -- $line
date -s "@$(( $2 / 1000 ))" >/dev/null
/usr/local/bin/guest-env "$3" > /run/guest.env || reboot -f
. /run/guest.env
{ cat /etc/pulse/default.pa.orig
  echo "load-module module-native-protocol-tcp port=4713 listen=0.0.0.0 auth-ip-acl=${PULSE_ALLOWED_IP}"; } > /run/pulse-default.pa
/usr/bin/supervisord -c /etc/guest/supervisord.conf &
while true; do wait || true; sleep 1; done
```

`apps/browser-slot/guest/bin/guest-env` (Python, mode 0755). It writes only the allowlisted keys, each validated and shell-quoted:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Decodes the start line's env (base64url JSON) into `export KEY='value'` lines; allowlisted keys only."""
import base64, json, re, shlex, sys

ALLOWED = {
    "NEKO_MEMBER_PROVIDER": r"^object$",
    "NEKO_MEMBER_OBJECT_USERS": r"^\[.{2,2000}\]$",
    "NEKO_WEBRTC_NAT1TO1": r"^[0-9.]{7,15}$",
    "NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX": r"^[0-9]{1,5}$",
    "NEKO_WEBRTC_TCPMUX": r"^[0-9]{1,5}$",
    "PULSE_ALLOWED_IP": r"^([0-9]{1,3}\.){3}[0-9]{1,3}(/[0-9]{1,2})?$",
}
data = json.loads(base64.urlsafe_b64decode(sys.argv[1] + "=" * (-len(sys.argv[1]) % 4)))
if set(data) != set(ALLOWED):
    sys.exit(1)
for key, pattern in ALLOWED.items():
    value = data[key]
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not re.match(pattern, value, re.S):
        sys.exit(1)
    print(f"export {key}={shlex.quote(value)}")
```

`apps/browser-slot/guest/programs/chromium.conf` is the slot's `supervisord/chromium.conf` `[program:chromium]` block with three changes:
- add `--proxy-server=http://192.168.127.1:3128` (the guest's only way out, spec §8);
- `environment=` adds `HOME="/home/neko",USER="neko",DISPLAY=":99.0"` literally, because there is no `%(ENV_…)s` in the guest;
- `user=neko`.

Keep the `[program:cdp-proxy]` and `[program:idle-probe]` blocks from `supervisord/chromium.conf` unchanged, in `cdp-proxy.conf` and `idle-probe.conf`. Then add:

```ini
; programs/pulseaudio.conf: the ACL comes from the start line (vm-init wrote /run/pulse-default.pa).
[program:pulseaudio]
command=/usr/bin/pulseaudio --log-level=error --disallow-module-loading --disallow-exit --exit-idle-time=-1 --file=/run/pulse-default.pa
user=neko
autorestart=true
priority=200

; programs/deskd.conf
[program:deskd]
command=/usr/bin/python3 /opt/deskd/deskd.py
environment=HOME="/home/neko",DISPLAY=":99.0",DBUS_SESSION_BUS_ADDRESS="unix:path=/run/user/1000/bus"
user=neko
autorestart=true
priority=900

; programs/chromium.conf (appended): Chromium exiting halts the guest, and the host restores a fresh VM.
[eventlistener:halt-on-chromium]
command=/usr/local/bin/halt-on-chromium
events=PROCESS_STATE_EXITED,PROCESS_STATE_FATAL
priority=100
```

`apps/browser-slot/guest/bin/halt-on-chromium` is `bin/exit-on-chromium` with the uptime wait removed (there is no Docker restart backoff in a VM) and the supervisord kill replaced by `reboot -f`. With `reboot=k` the VMM exits, and the controller restores at once (Task P1.10). That keeps "a new browser id implies a host restore" true.

The guest's `[program:neko]` is the slot image's own, extracted at build time (Step 7). It reads the `NEKO_*` variables from `/run/guest.env` through supervisord's inherited environment, because vm-init sources the file before it starts supervisord.

- [ ] **Step 7: Add the `guest` stage** to `apps/browser-slot/Dockerfile`, between `slot-image` and the final `FROM slot-image AS slot`:

```dockerfile
# D53c guest (spec §6.1): the slot image plus a PID 1, the guest programs and deskd. Built into a
# read-only ext4 root by the `rootfs` stage; never run as a container.
FROM slot-image AS guest
RUN set -eux; apt-get update; apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends python3 iproute2; \
    apt-get clean; rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/* /var/cache/apt/*; \
    mkdir -p /mnt/rw /etc/guest/programs /opt/deskd
COPY --chmod=0755 guest/vm-init /sbin/vm-init
COPY --chmod=0755 guest/bin/halt-on-chromium guest/bin/guest-env /usr/local/bin/
COPY guest/supervisord.conf /etc/guest/supervisord.conf
COPY guest/programs/ /etc/guest/programs/
COPY guest/deskd/ /opt/deskd/
# n.eko's own [program:neko] block, verbatim from the image (P0 recorded which file holds it).
RUN set -eux; \
    awk '/^\[program:neko\]/{p=1;print;next} /^\[/{p=0} p' /etc/neko/supervisord.conf /etc/neko/supervisord/*.conf > /etc/guest/programs/neko.conf; \
    grep -q '^\[program:neko\]' /etc/guest/programs/neko.conf; \
    cd /opt/deskd && python3 -m unittest -q && rm -rf __pycache__ test_deskd.py
```

Expected: both builds succeed, the guest build's unittest passes, and `verify.sh` (which builds the default target) passes unchanged.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing verify script.** `apps/browser-slot/test/verify-desktop.sh` (mode 0755). It runs on the Dokploy host only, after H2:

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Desktop-slot image checks (D53c spec §5): runs the image as compose would and asserts the
# container's privileges, its ruleset and its VM. Host only (needs /dev/kvm; user-approved H2).
set -euo pipefail
HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." && pwd)"; ROOT="$(cd "$HERE/../.." && pwd)"
IMAGE="mastertutor/desktop-slot:verify-$$"; NAME="mt-desktop-verify-$$"
cleanup() { docker rm -f "$NAME" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; docker image rm -f "$IMAGE" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; }
trap cleanup EXIT
docker build -q --label mastertutor.ci=1 --target desktop-slot --build-context repo="$ROOT" -t "$IMAGE" "$HERE" >/dev/null
KVM_GID="$(stat -c %g /dev/kvm)"
docker run -d --name "$NAME" --label mastertutor.ci=1 --init --read-only \
  --device /dev/kvm --device /dev/net/tun --cap-drop ALL --cap-add NET_ADMIN --cap-add SETUID --cap-add SETGID \
  --group-add "$KVM_GID" --security-opt no-new-privileges:true --memory 10g --memory-swap 10g --cpus 4 --pids-limit 256 \
  --sysctl net.ipv6.conf.all.disable_ipv6=1 --sysctl net.ipv6.conf.default.disable_ipv6=1 --sysctl net.ipv4.ip_forward=1 \
  --tmpfs /run/vm:size=6g,mode=0700,uid=10001 --tmpfs /tmp:size=64m \
  -e SLOT_NAME=desktop-1 -e KVM_GID="$KVM_GID" -e CDP_ALLOWED_IP=172.17.0.1 -e PULSE_ALLOWED_IP=172.17.0.1 \
  -e NEKO_ALLOWED_IPS=172.17.0.1 -e NEKO_WEBRTC_NAT1TO1=127.0.0.1 -e NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX=59101 -e NEKO_WEBRTC_TCPMUX=59101 \
  -e NEKO_ADMIN_SECRET="$(openssl rand -hex 24)" -e NEKO_MEMBER_SECRET="$(openssl rand -hex 24)" "$IMAGE" >/dev/null
fail() { echo "verify-desktop: $*" >&2; docker logs "$NAME" 2>&1 | tail -50 >&2; exit 1; }
for _ in $(seq 1 120); do docker logs "$NAME" 2>&1 | grep -q '"desktop host ready"' && break; sleep 1; done
docker logs "$NAME" 2>&1 | grep -q '"desktop host ready"' || fail "never became ready"
x() { docker exec "$NAME" "$@"; }
# Privileges: the VMM runs as uid fc with no capabilities and seccomp-filtered.
fcpid="$(x pgrep -x firecracker)"; [[ -n "$fcpid" ]] || fail "no firecracker"
x sh -c "grep -q 'CapEff:\s*0000000000000000' /proc/$fcpid/status" || fail "firecracker has capabilities"
x sh -c "grep -q 'Seccomp:\s*2' /proc/$fcpid/status" || fail "firecracker is not seccomp-filtered"
[[ "$(x stat -c %u /proc/$fcpid)" == 10001 ]] || fail "firecracker is not uid fc"
x sh -c 'test "$(cat /sys/fs/cgroup/memory.swap.max)" = 0' || fail "guest memory may swap"
x sh -c 'touch /etc/x 2>/dev/null' && fail "root filesystem is writable"
# Ruleset: guest forwarding is dropped; only the proxy uid may leave; agent-only ports.
rules="$(x nft list ruleset)"
grep -q 'iifname "tap0" drop' <<<"$rules" || fail "guest forwarding is not dropped"
grep -q 'meta skuid "proxy"' <<<"$rules" || fail "egress is not limited to the proxy uid"
grep -q 'tcp dport { 9225, 9226 } accept' <<<"$rules" || fail "agent-only ports missing"
# The guest's browser answers CDP through the DNAT (from inside the container namespace).
x node -e "fetch('http://192.168.127.2:9223/json/version').then((r) => r.text()).then((t) => process.stdout.write(t))" \
  | grep -q webSocketDebuggerUrl || fail "no CDP from the guest"
echo "verify-desktop: ok"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Move the shared shell helpers.** Move `validate_allow_cidr`, `hmac_hex` and `profile` out of `slot-entrypoint` and into `apps/browser-slot/bin/slot-lib.sh`, byte for byte. Add one function built from the inline `NEKO_MEMBER_OBJECT_USERS` construction:

```bash
# neko_members_json <admin_password> <user_password>: the n.eko member object (spec §10.3 control lock).
neko_members_json() {
  printf '[{"username":"agent","password":"%s","profile":%s},{"username":"user","password":"%s","profile":%s}]' \
    "$1" "$(profile agent true true true true)" "$2" "$(profile user false false false false)"
}
```

`slot-entrypoint` then does `. /usr/local/bin/slot-lib.sh` and `NEKO_MEMBER_OBJECT_USERS="$(neko_members_json "$admin_password" "$user_password")"`. Add `bin/slot-lib.sh` to the slot image's existing `COPY --chmod=0755 bin/…` line. `verify.sh` must still pass unchanged.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the nftables template.** `apps/desktop-host/nft/desktop.nft`. The entrypoint substitutes `@CDP@`, `@NEKO@`, `@PULSE@`, `@MUX@` and `@FIXTURES@` after validation.

```
# D53c spec §5.3. Container namespace, outside the guest: the guest cannot change any of this.
flush ruleset
define GUEST = 192.168.127.2
define PRIVATE = { 0.0.0.0/8, 10.0.0.0/8, 100.64.0.0/10, 127.0.0.0/8, 169.254.0.0/16, 172.16.0.0/12, 192.0.0.0/24, 192.0.2.0/24, 192.168.0.0/16, 198.18.0.0/15, 198.51.100.0/24, 203.0.113.0/24, 224.0.0.0/4, 240.0.0.0/4 }
table ip mt {
  chain prerouting {
    type nat hook prerouting priority dstnat; policy accept;
    iifname "tap0" return
    ip saddr @CDP@ tcp dport { 9223, 9224 } dnat to $GUEST
    ip saddr { @NEKO@ } tcp dport 8080 dnat to $GUEST
    ip saddr @PULSE@ tcp dport 4713 dnat to $GUEST
    meta l4proto { tcp, udp } th dport @MUX@ dnat to $GUEST
  }
  chain input {
    type filter hook input priority filter; policy drop;
    iif "lo" accept
    ct state established,related accept
    ip saddr @CDP@ tcp dport { 9225, 9226 } accept
    iifname "tap0" ip daddr 192.168.127.1 tcp dport 3128 accept
  }
  chain forward {
    type filter hook forward priority filter; policy drop;
    ct status dnat accept
    iifname "tap0" drop
  }
  chain output {
    type filter hook output priority filter; policy drop;
    oif "lo" accept
    ct state established,related accept
    oifname "tap0" accept
    meta skuid "proxy" ip daddr { @FIXTURES@ } accept
    meta skuid "proxy" ip daddr $PRIVATE reject
    meta skuid "proxy" meta l4proto tcp accept
    meta skuid "proxy" udp dport 53 ip daddr 127.0.0.11 accept
    meta skuid "proxy" tcp dport 53 ip daddr 127.0.0.11 accept
  }
}
table ip6 mt6 {
  chain any { type filter hook forward priority filter; policy drop; }
  chain in { type filter hook input priority filter; policy drop; iif "lo" accept; }
  chain out { type filter hook output priority filter; policy drop; oif "lo" accept; }
}
```

Docker's embedded DNS (`127.0.0.11`) answers on loopback inside the container, so the proxy can resolve. `@FIXTURES@` is `SLOT_EGRESS_ALLOW_CIDRS`, or `192.0.2.255/32` (TEST-NET, never routed) when that is empty. The `oif "lo" accept` rule already admits DNS to `127.0.0.11`; the two explicit DNS lines document it, and P4 checks it.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the entrypoint.** `apps/desktop-host/bin/desktop-entrypoint` (mode 0755):

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# Desktop-slot entrypoint (D53c spec §5.2). Root only long enough to validate the environment, create
# tap0 for uid fc and load nftables; then the egress proxy (uid proxy) and the controller (uid fc)
# run with no capabilities. If either exits, the container exits and Compose restarts it.
set -euo pipefail
. /usr/local/bin/slot-lib.sh
for name in SLOT_NAME NEKO_ADMIN_SECRET NEKO_MEMBER_SECRET CDP_ALLOWED_IP PULSE_ALLOWED_IP NEKO_ALLOWED_IPS \
  NEKO_WEBRTC_NAT1TO1 NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX NEKO_WEBRTC_TCPMUX KVM_GID; do
  if [[ -z "${!name:-}" ]]; then echo "desktop-entrypoint: $name is required" >&2; exit 64; fi
done
[[ "$SLOT_NAME" =~ ^desktop-[1-9][0-9]?$ ]] || { echo "desktop-entrypoint: invalid SLOT_NAME" >&2; exit 64; }
ip_or_cidr='^([0-9]{1,3}\.){3}[0-9]{1,3}(/([0-9]|[12][0-9]|3[0-2]))?$'
for value in "$CDP_ALLOWED_IP" "$PULSE_ALLOWED_IP" ${NEKO_ALLOWED_IPS//,/ }; do
  [[ "$value" =~ $ip_or_cidr ]] || { echo "desktop-entrypoint: invalid address $value" >&2; exit 64; }
done
[[ "$NEKO_WEBRTC_NAT1TO1" =~ ^([0-9]{1,3}\.){3}[0-9]{1,3}$ ]] || { echo "desktop-entrypoint: invalid NAT1TO1" >&2; exit 64; }
[[ "$NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX" =~ ^[0-9]{1,5}$ && "$NEKO_WEBRTC_TCPMUX" == "$NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX" ]] || { echo "desktop-entrypoint: invalid mux" >&2; exit 64; }
[[ "$KVM_GID" =~ ^[0-9]{1,7}$ ]] || { echo "desktop-entrypoint: invalid KVM_GID" >&2; exit 64; }
fixtures="192.0.2.255/32"
if [[ -n "${SLOT_EGRESS_ALLOW_CIDRS:-}" ]]; then
  for cidr in ${SLOT_EGRESS_ALLOW_CIDRS//,/ }; do validate_allow_cidr "$cidr" || { echo "desktop-entrypoint: invalid SLOT_EGRESS_ALLOW_CIDRS" >&2; exit 64; }; done
  fixtures="${SLOT_EGRESS_ALLOW_CIDRS//,/, }"
fi
(( ${#NEKO_ADMIN_SECRET} >= 32 && ${#NEKO_MEMBER_SECRET} >= 32 )) || { echo "desktop-entrypoint: n.eko secrets must be at least 32 characters" >&2; exit 64; }

admin_password="$(hmac_hex "$NEKO_ADMIN_SECRET")"; user_password="$(hmac_hex "$NEKO_MEMBER_SECRET")"
unset NEKO_ADMIN_SECRET NEKO_MEMBER_SECRET
GUEST_ENV_JSON="$(printf '{"NEKO_MEMBER_PROVIDER":"object","NEKO_MEMBER_OBJECT_USERS":%s,"NEKO_WEBRTC_NAT1TO1":"%s","NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX":"%s","NEKO_WEBRTC_TCPMUX":"%s","PULSE_ALLOWED_IP":"%s"}' \
  "$(neko_members_json "$admin_password" "$user_password" | sed 's/"/\\"/g; s/^/"/; s/$/"/')" \
  "$NEKO_WEBRTC_NAT1TO1" "$NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX" "$NEKO_WEBRTC_TCPMUX" "$PULSE_ALLOWED_IP")"
unset admin_password user_password

ip tuntap add dev tap0 mode tap user fc
ip addr add 192.168.127.1/30 dev tap0
ip link set tap0 up
sed -e "s|@CDP@|$CDP_ALLOWED_IP|g" -e "s|@NEKO@|${NEKO_ALLOWED_IPS//,/, }|g" -e "s|@PULSE@|$PULSE_ALLOWED_IP|g" \
    -e "s|@MUX@|$NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX|g" -e "s|@FIXTURES@|$fixtures|g" /etc/desktop/desktop.nft > /tmp/desktop.nft
nft -f /tmp/desktop.nft
rm -f /tmp/desktop.nft

drop() { local uid="$1" groups="$2"; shift 2
  setpriv --reuid="$uid" --regid="$uid" --groups="$groups" --inh-caps=-all --bounding-set=-all --no-new-privs "$@"; }
cd /opt/desktop-host
SLOT_EGRESS_ALLOW_CIDRS="${SLOT_EGRESS_ALLOW_CIDRS:-}" drop 10002 10002 node apps/desktop-host/src/egress/main.ts &
GUEST_ENV_JSON="$GUEST_ENV_JSON" drop 10001 "10001,$KVM_GID" node apps/desktop-host/src/main.ts &
unset GUEST_ENV_JSON
wait -n
exit 1
```

In `apps/desktop-host/src/main.ts`, read and validate the guest env once, and send it with every start line. The guest's `guest-env` re-validates it.

```ts
const guestEnv = Buffer.from(
  JSON.stringify(
    z.object({
      NEKO_MEMBER_PROVIDER: z.literal("object"),
      NEKO_MEMBER_OBJECT_USERS: z.string().min(2).max(2_000),
      NEKO_WEBRTC_NAT1TO1: z.string().regex(/^[0-9.]{7,15}$/),
      NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]{1,5}$/),
      NEKO_WEBRTC_TCPMUX: z.string().regex(/^[0-9]{1,5}$/),
      PULSE_ALLOWED_IP: z.string().regex(/^([0-9]{1,3}\.){3}[0-9]{1,3}(\/[0-9]{1,2})?$/),
    }).strict().parse(JSON.parse(process.env.GUEST_ENV_JSON ?? "{}")),
  ),
).toString("base64url");
delete process.env.GUEST_ENV_JSON;
// … in sendStart:
    socket.end(`start ${epochMs} ${guestEnv}\n`);
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Add the remaining stages.** Insert these before the final `FROM slot-image AS slot`. The `ARG` defaults are the P0 pins.

```dockerfile
# Guest kernel (spec §6.1): Linux 6.1 LTS, Firecracker's CI config plus vsock and VMGenID; no modules.
FROM debian:trixie-slim@sha256:<P0 pin> AS kernel
ARG KERNEL_VERSION=<P0 pin>
ARG KERNEL_SHA256=<P0 pin>
RUN set -eux; apt-get update; apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends build-essential bc flex bison libelf-dev ca-certificates curl xz-utils; \
    curl -fsSLo /tmp/linux.tar.xz "https://cdn.kernel.org/pub/linux/kernel/v6.x/linux-${KERNEL_VERSION}.tar.xz"; \
    echo "${KERNEL_SHA256}  /tmp/linux.tar.xz" | sha256sum -c -; mkdir /src; tar -xJf /tmp/linux.tar.xz -C /src --strip-components=1
COPY guest/kernel/ /kcfg/
RUN set -eux; cd /src; ./scripts/kconfig/merge_config.sh -m /kcfg/microvm-kernel-ci-x86_64-6.1.config /kcfg/mt.config; \
    make olddefconfig; for opt in VIRTIO_VSOCKETS VMGENID OVERLAY_FS; do grep -q "^CONFIG_${opt}=y" .config; done; \
    make -j"$(nproc)" vmlinux; cp vmlinux /vmlinux

# Read-only guest root (spec §6.1): the guest stage's filesystem as ext4, sized to fit plus 10%.
FROM debian:trixie-slim@sha256:<P0 pin> AS rootfs
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends e2fsprogs && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
COPY --from=guest / /tree
RUN set -eux; rm -rf /tree/proc/* /tree/sys/* /tree/dev/* /tree/tmp/* /tree/run/*; \
    size=$(( $(du -sm /tree | cut -f1) * 11 / 10 + 64 )); \
    mkfs.ext4 -q -d /tree -L mtguest -E root_owner=0:0 /rootfs.ext4 "${size}M"; rm -rf /tree

# The desktop host's Node code: the same pnpm recipe as the root Dockerfile's runtime-build, filtered
# to @mastertutor/desktop-host. Workspace packages stay symlinked outside node_modules (type stripping).
FROM node:24-slim@sha256:d6aa754f16b3197301076f047b5def2f02ea1dbbc2ca920407d46d7ec7f87b20 AS desktop-host-deps
ENV CI=true
RUN npm install -g pnpm@10.34.6 && npm cache clean --force
WORKDIR /repo
COPY --from=repo pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm-workspace.yaml package.json tsconfig.base.json ./
COPY --from=repo packages/contracts packages/contracts
COPY --from=repo packages/net-policy packages/net-policy
COPY --from=repo apps/desktop-host apps/desktop-host
RUN pnpm install --frozen-lockfile --prod --filter "@mastertutor/desktop-host..." \
 && find apps/desktop-host packages -path '*/node_modules' -prune -o -name '*.test.ts' -print0 | xargs -0 rm -f

# D53c desktop slot (spec §5): Firecracker, nftables, the controller and the proxy. Unprivileged.
FROM debian:trixie-slim@sha256:<P0 pin> AS desktop-slot
ARG FIRECRACKER_VERSION=<P0 pin>
ARG FIRECRACKER_SHA256=<P0 pin>
RUN set -eux; apt-get update; apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends ca-certificates curl nftables iproute2 util-linux openssl; \
    curl -fsSLo /tmp/fc.tgz "https://github.com/firecracker-microvm/firecracker/releases/download/${FIRECRACKER_VERSION}/firecracker-${FIRECRACKER_VERSION}-x86_64.tgz"; \
    echo "${FIRECRACKER_SHA256}  /tmp/fc.tgz" | sha256sum -c -; tar -xzf /tmp/fc.tgz -C /tmp; \
    install -m 0755 /tmp/release-*/firecracker-"${FIRECRACKER_VERSION}"-x86_64 /usr/local/bin/firecracker; \
    apt-get purge -y curl; apt-get autoremove -y; rm -rf /tmp/* /var/lib/apt/lists/*; \
    useradd -u 10001 -M -s /usr/sbin/nologin fc; useradd -u 10002 -M -s /usr/sbin/nologin proxy
COPY --from=node:24-slim@sha256:d6aa754f16b3197301076f047b5def2f02ea1dbbc2ca920407d46d7ec7f87b20 /usr/local/bin/node /usr/local/bin/node
COPY --from=desktop-host-deps /repo /opt/desktop-host
COPY --from=kernel /vmlinux /opt/vm/vmlinux
COPY --from=rootfs /rootfs.ext4 /opt/vm/rootfs.ext4
COPY --chmod=0755 bin/slot-lib.sh /usr/local/bin/slot-lib.sh
COPY --from=repo --chmod=0755 apps/desktop-host/bin/desktop-entrypoint /usr/local/bin/desktop-entrypoint
COPY --from=repo apps/desktop-host/nft/desktop.nft /etc/desktop/desktop.nft
HEALTHCHECK --interval=5s --timeout=3s --start-period=60s --retries=6 CMD ["sh", "-c", "exec 3<>/dev/tcp/127.0.0.1/9226"]
ENTRYPOINT ["/usr/local/bin/desktop-entrypoint"]
```

```
CONFIG_VIRTIO_VSOCKETS_COMMON=y
CONFIG_VMGENID=y
CONFIG_OVERLAY_FS=y
CONFIG_TMPFS=y
CONFIG_DEVTMPFS=y
CONFIG_MODULES=n
CONFIG_IPV6=n
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { encodeFrame, FrameReader, FramingError } from "./framing.ts";

describe("desk framing", () => {
  it("round-trips headers and payloads split across chunks", () => {
    const wire = Buffer.concat([
      encodeFrame({ id: 1, ok: true, result: {}, bytes: 3 }, Buffer.from("abc")),
      encodeFrame({ id: 2, ok: true, result: {}, bytes: 0 }),
    ]);
    const reader = new FrameReader();
    const frames = [...wire].flatMap((byte) => reader.push(Buffer.from([byte])));
    expect(frames.map((f) => [(f.header as { id: number }).id, f.payload.toString()])).toEqual([[1, "abc"], [2, ""]]);
  });

  it("rejects an oversized header, a bad JSON header and an oversized payload", () => {
    expect(() => new FrameReader({ headerBytes: 16, payloadBytes: 10 }).push(Buffer.from(`${"x".repeat(20)}\n`))).toThrow(FramingError);
    expect(() => new FrameReader().push(Buffer.from("{nope\n"))).toThrow(FramingError);
    expect(() => new FrameReader({ headerBytes: 100, payloadBytes: 10 }).push(Buffer.from('{"bytes":11}\n'))).toThrow(FramingError);
  });

  it("refuses to encode a header whose bytes disagree with the payload", () => {
    expect(() => encodeFrame({ id: 1, bytes: 2 }, Buffer.from("abc"))).toThrow(FramingError);
  });
});
```

`apps/agent/src/desk/client.test.ts` runs a fake `deskd` on loopback:

```ts
import { once } from "node:events";
import { createServer, type AddressInfo, type Server, type Socket } from "node:net";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createDeskClient, DeskRefused, DeskUnavailable } from "./client.ts";
import { encodeFrame, FrameReader } from "./framing.ts";

const servers: Server[] = [];
afterEach(() => servers.splice(0).forEach((server) => server.close()));

async function fakeDeskd(answer: (header: { id: number; method: string; params: unknown }, socket: Socket) => void) {
  const server = createServer((socket) => {
    const reader = new FrameReader();
    socket.on("data", (chunk) => reader.push(chunk).forEach((frame) => answer(frame.header as never, socket)));
  });
  servers.push(server);
  server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1");
  await once(server, "listening");
  const port = (server.address() as AddressInfo).port;
  return createDeskClient({ endpoint: async () => ({ host: "127.0.0.1", port }), timeoutMs: 500 });
}

describe("DeskClient", () => {
  it("says hello and validates the answer", async () => {
    const desk = await fakeDeskd(({ id }, socket) =>
      socket.write(encodeFrame({ id, ok: true, result: { methods: ["hello", "fileGet"], version: "1" }, bytes: 0 })),
    );
    expect(await desk.hello()).toEqual({ methods: ["hello", "fileGet"], version: "1" });
    desk.close();
  });

  it("returns file bytes only when their size matches the stated size and the cap", async () => {
    const desk = await fakeDeskd(({ id, params }, socket) => {
      const lie = (params as { path: string }).path.endsWith("lie");
      socket.write(encodeFrame({ id, ok: true, result: { size: lie ? 2 : 3 }, bytes: 3 }, Buffer.from("abc")));
    });
    expect((await desk.fileGet("/home/neko/a", 10)).toString()).toBe("abc");
    await expect(desk.fileGet("/home/neko/lie", 10)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(DeskUnavailable);
    await expect(desk.fileGet("/home/neko/a", 2)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(DeskUnavailable);
    desk.close();
  });

  it("refuses a path outside /home/neko before sending anything", async () => {
    let asked = 0;
    const desk = await fakeDeskd(() => void (asked += 1));
    await expect(desk.fileGet("/etc/passwd", 10)).rejects.toThrow();
    expect(asked).toBe(0);
    desk.close();
  });

  it("maps deskd errors to DeskRefused with the code", async () => {
    const desk = await fakeDeskd(({ id }, socket) =>
      socket.write(encodeFrame({ id, ok: false, error: { code: "too_large", message: "big" }, bytes: 0 })),
    );
    await expect(desk.fileGet("/home/neko/a", 10)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "too_large" });
    await expect(desk.fileGet("/home/neko/a", 10)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(DeskRefused);
    desk.close();
  });

  it("treats a schema-breaking answer, garbage or silence as unavailable", async () => {
    const liar = await fakeDeskd(({ id }, socket) =>
      socket.write(encodeFrame({ id, ok: true, result: { methods: Array(40).fill("x"), version: "1" }, bytes: 0 })),
    );
    await expect(liar.hello()).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(DeskUnavailable);
    liar.close();
    const garbage = await fakeDeskd((_h, socket) => socket.write("not json\n"));
    await expect(garbage.hello()).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(DeskUnavailable);
    garbage.close();
    const silent = await fakeDeskd(() => undefined);
    await expect(silent.hello()).rejects.toMatchObject({ reason: "timeout" });
    silent.close();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the framing.** `apps/agent/src/desk/framing.ts`:

```ts
import { DESK_LIMITS } from "@mastertutor/contracts";

export class FramingError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "FramingError";
  }
}

/** One JSON header line, then exactly header.bytes raw bytes (spec §7.2). */
export function encodeFrame(header: Record<string, unknown>, payload: Uint8Array = new Uint8Array()): Buffer {
  if ((header.bytes ?? 0) !== payload.length) throw new FramingError("header bytes disagree with the payload");
  return Buffer.concat([Buffer.from(`${JSON.stringify(header)}\n`), payload]);
}

/** Incremental decoder; throws FramingError on anything over the limits or not JSON. */
export class FrameReader {
  readonly #limits: { headerBytes: number; payloadBytes: number };
  #buffer = Buffer.alloc(0);
  #pending: { header: unknown; bytes: number } | null = null;

  constructor(limits: { headerBytes: number; payloadBytes: number } = DESK_LIMITS) {
    this.#limits = limits;
  }

  push(chunk: Buffer): Array<{ header: unknown; payload: Buffer }> {
    this.#buffer = Buffer.concat([this.#buffer, chunk]);
    const frames: Array<{ header: unknown; payload: Buffer }> = [];
    for (;;) {
      if (!this.#pending) {
        const end = this.#buffer.indexOf(0x0a);
        if (end < 0) {
          if (this.#buffer.length > this.#limits.headerBytes) throw new FramingError("header too long");
          return frames;
        }
        if (end > this.#limits.headerBytes) throw new FramingError("header too long");
        let header: unknown;
        try {
          header = JSON.parse(this.#buffer.subarray(0, end).toString("utf8"));
        } catch {
          throw new FramingError("header is not JSON");
        }
        const bytes = (header as { bytes?: unknown } | null)?.bytes ?? 0;
        if (typeof bytes !== "number" || !Number.isInteger(bytes) || bytes < 0 || bytes > this.#limits.payloadBytes)
          throw new FramingError("invalid payload size");
        this.#pending = { header, bytes };
        this.#buffer = this.#buffer.subarray(end + 1);
      }
      if (this.#buffer.length < this.#pending.bytes) return frames;
      frames.push({ header: this.#pending.header, payload: Buffer.from(this.#buffer.subarray(0, this.#pending.bytes)) });
      this.#buffer = this.#buffer.subarray(this.#pending.bytes);
      this.#pending = null;
    }
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the client.** `apps/agent/src/desk/client.ts`:

```ts
import { connect, type Socket } from "node:net";
import {
  DESK_PARAMS,
  DESK_RESULTS,
  DeskResponseHeader,
  type DeskMethod,
} from "@mastertutor/contracts";
import type { z } from "zod";
import { encodeFrame, FrameReader, FramingError } from "./framing.ts";

/** deskd could not be asked, or answered something the contract does not allow (untrusted guest). */
export class DeskUnavailable extends Error {
  readonly reason: string;
  constructor(reason: string) {
    super(`desk unavailable: ${reason}`);
    this.name = "DeskUnavailable";
    this.reason = reason;
  }
}

/** deskd refused the call with a typed code. */
export class DeskRefused extends Error {
  readonly code: string;
  constructor(code: string, message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "DeskRefused";
    this.code = code;
  }
}

export interface DeskClient {
  hello(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<z.infer<(typeof DESK_RESULTS)["hello"]>>;
  fileGet(path: string, maxBytes: number, signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<Buffer>;
  close(): void;
}

interface Pending {
  method: DeskMethod;
  resolve(value: { result: unknown; payload: Buffer }): void;
  reject(error: Error): void;
  timer: NodeJS.Timeout;
}

/** One lazy connection per client (one per lease); calls are multiplexed by id. */
export function createDeskClient(options: {
  /** Resolved on each (re)connect: the slot's relay address (DESK_PORT in production). */
  endpoint(): Promise<{ host: string; port: number }>;
  timeoutMs?: number;
}): DeskClient {
  let socket: Promise<Socket> | null = null;
  let nextId = 1;
  const pending = new Map<number, Pending>();

  const failAll = (reason: string) => {
    for (const [id, call] of pending) {
      clearTimeout(call.timer);
      call.reject(new DeskUnavailable(reason));
      pending.delete(id);
    }
  };
  const drop = (reason: string) => {
    failAll(reason);
    void socket?.then((s) => s.destroy()).catch(() => undefined);
    socket = null;
  };

  const open = async (): Promise<Socket> => {
    const s = connect(await options.endpoint());
    const reader = new FrameReader();
    s.on("data", (chunk: Buffer) => {
      let frames;
      try {
        frames = reader.push(chunk);
      } catch (error) {
        return drop(error instanceof FramingError ? "bad_frame" : "bad_frame");
      }
      for (const frame of frames) {
        const header = DeskResponseHeader.safeParse(frame.header);
        if (!header.success) return drop("bad_header");
        const call = pending.get(header.data.id);
        if (!call) return drop("unknown_id");
        pending.delete(header.data.id);
        clearTimeout(call.timer);
        if (!header.data.ok) call.reject(new DeskRefused(header.data.error.code, header.data.error.message));
        else {
          const result = DESK_RESULTS[call.method].safeParse(header.data.result);
          if (!result.success) call.reject(new DeskUnavailable("bad_result"));
          else call.resolve({ result: result.data, payload: frame.payload });
        }
      }
    });
    s.on("error", () => drop("connection"));
    s.on("close", () => drop("closed"));
    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
      s.once("connect", resolve);
      s.once("error", reject);
    });
    return s;
  };

  async function call<M extends DeskMethod>(method: M, params: unknown, signal?: AbortSignal) {
    const parsed = DESK_PARAMS[method].parse(params);
    socket ??= open();
    let s: Socket;
    try {
      s = await socket;
    } catch {
      socket = null;
      throw new DeskUnavailable("connection");
    }
    const id = nextId++;
    return new Promise<{ result: unknown; payload: Buffer }>((resolve, reject) => {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        pending.delete(id);
        reject(new DeskUnavailable("timeout"));
      }, options.timeoutMs ?? 10_000);
      pending.set(id, { method, resolve, reject, timer });
      signal?.addEventListener("abort", () => {
        if (pending.delete(id)) {
          clearTimeout(timer);
          reject(new DeskUnavailable("aborted"));
        }
      }, { once: true });
      s.write(encodeFrame({ id, method, params: parsed, bytes: 0 }));
    });
  }

  return {
    async hello(signal) {
      return (await call("hello", {}, signal)).result as z.infer<(typeof DESK_RESULTS)["hello"]>;
    },
    async fileGet(path, maxBytes, signal) {
      const { result, payload } = await call("fileGet", { path, maxBytes }, signal);
      const { size } = result as { size: number };
      if (payload.length !== size || size > maxBytes) throw new DeskUnavailable("bad_size");
      return payload;
    },
    close: () => drop("closed"),
  };
}
```

```ts
const endpoints = async () => ({ host: "10.0.0.9", cdpPort: 9223, deskPort: 9225, controlPort: 9226 });
const vm = (vmId: string | null) => new Response(JSON.stringify({ vmId }), { status: 200 });

      desktopSlotProvider({ endpoints, control: control(() => cdp), fetch: async () => vm(vmId) });

    const first = await provider.readBrowserId("desktop-1");

    await expect(bad.recycle("desktop-2")).rejects.toThrow();
  });
});
```

`apps/agent/src/slots/desktop-provider.ts`:

```ts
import { lookup } from "node:dns/promises";
import {
  CDP_PROXY_PORT,
  DESK_PORT,
  DESKTOP_CONTROL_PORT,
  RecycleResult,
  SlotName,
  VmState,
} from "@mastertutor/contracts";
import { createDeskClient, type DeskClient } from "../desk/client.ts";
import { cdpBrowserControl, type BrowserControl } from "./lifecycle.ts";
import type { SlotProvider } from "./provider.ts";

export interface DesktopEndpoints {
  host: string;
  cdpPort: number;
  deskPort: number;
  controlPort: number;
}

/** Production: the slot's cdp-network IP (Chrome rejects hostnames in Host) and the fixed ports. */
async function defaultEndpoints(name: string): Promise<DesktopEndpoints> {
  const host = (await lookup(SlotName.parse(name), { family: 4 })).address;
  return { host, cdpPort: CDP_PROXY_PORT, deskPort: DESK_PORT, controlPort: DESKTOP_CONTROL_PORT };
}

/**
 * Desktop slots (D53c §9.1). The readiness id is the controller's vmId, which changes only on a
 * host-side restore, and is returned only once the guest's CDP answers. So a guest that restarts
 * Chromium by itself can never make SlotPool skip the restore (no leftover state across runs).
 */
export function desktopSlotProvider(
  options: { endpoints?(name: string): Promise<DesktopEndpoints>; control?: BrowserControl; fetch?: typeof fetch } = {},
): SlotProvider & { desk(name: string): DeskClient } {
  const endpoints = options.endpoints ?? defaultEndpoints;
  const control = options.control ?? cdpBrowserControl;
  const http = options.fetch ?? fetch;
  const cdpBaseUrl = async (name: string) => {
    const at = await endpoints(name);
    return `http://${at.host}:${at.cdpPort}`;
  };
  const controlUrl = async (name: string, path: string) => {
    const at = await endpoints(name);
    return `http://${at.host}:${at.controlPort}${path}`;
  };
  return {
    kind: "desktop",
    cdpBaseUrl,
    async readBrowserId(name) {
      try {
        const [state, cdpId] = await Promise.all([
          http(await controlUrl(name, "/vm"), { signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2_000) }).then(async (r) =>
            VmState.parse(await r.json()),
          ),
          cdpBaseUrl(name).then((url) => control.readBrowserId(url)),
        ]);
        return state.vmId && cdpId ? state.vmId : null;
      } catch {
        return null;
      }
    },
    async recycle(name) {
      const response = await http(await controlUrl(name, "/recycle"), {
        method: "POST",
        signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30_000),
      });
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`desktop recycle failed: ${response.status}`);
      RecycleResult.parse(await response.json());
    },
    desk: (name) =>
      createDeskClient({
        endpoint: async () => {
          const at = await endpoints(name);
          return { host: at.host, port: at.deskPort };
        },
      }),
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** In `download-gate.ts`:

```ts
export interface DownloadFolder {
  slotPath: string;
  /** The same folder on the agent's mount of the volume; null when it has none (tests). */
  localPath: string | null;
  /**
   * Desktop slots (D53c §12.4) share no volume: copies <slotPath>/<id> into <localPath>/<id>
   * (deskd fileGet) before the download is announced. Absent for browser slots.
   */
  pull?(id: string): Promise<void>;
}
```

Replace `#announce`:

```ts
  #announce(guid: string, by: FinishedDownload["by"]): void {
    const source = this.#letThrough.get(guid);
    if (!source) return;
    const announce = () => this.#finished?.({ id: guid, ...source, by });
    const pull = this.#folder?.pull;
    if (!pull) return announce();
    void pull(guid).then(announce, () =>
      this.#log.warn({ errorCode: "download_pull_failed" }, "a finished download could not be copied from the slot"),
    );
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** In `compose.yml`, after `x-browser-slot`:

```yaml
# D53c desktop slot (spec §5.1): one Firecracker VM per service. The only device access is KVM and
# TUN; NET_ADMIN is the only capability added beyond Docker's defaults, and SETUID/SETGID are the
# only defaults kept (the entrypoint drops to uid fc and uid proxy). No AppArmor profile is needed.
x-desktop-slot: &desktop-slot
  image: mastertutor/desktop-slot:local
  build:
    context: ./apps/browser-slot
    target: desktop-slot
    additional_contexts:
      repo: .
  profiles: ["desktop"]
  restart: always
  init: true
  read_only: true
  devices:
    - /dev/kvm
    - /dev/net/tun
  group_add:
    - ${KVM_GID:?set KVM_GID}
  cap_drop: [ALL]
  cap_add: [NET_ADMIN, SETUID, SETGID]
  security_opt:
    - no-new-privileges:true
  sysctls:
    net.ipv6.conf.all.disable_ipv6: 1
    net.ipv6.conf.default.disable_ipv6: 1
    net.ipv4.ip_forward: 1
  tmpfs:
    - /run/vm:size=6g,mode=0700,uid=10001
    - /tmp:size=64m
  mem_limit: 10g
  memswap_limit: 10g
  cpus: 4
  pids_limit: 256
  networks:
    cdp: {}
    egress: {}
    pulse: {}
```

Under `services`, after `browser-6`:

```yaml
  desktop-1:
    <<: *desktop-slot
    environment:
      <<: *slot-env
      SLOT_NAME: desktop-1
      KVM_GID: ${KVM_GID:?set KVM_GID}
      NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX: "59101"
      NEKO_WEBRTC_TCPMUX: "59101"
    ports: ["59101:59101/udp", "59101:59101/tcp"]

  desktop-2:
    <<: *desktop-slot
    environment:
      <<: *slot-env
      SLOT_NAME: desktop-2
      KVM_GID: ${KVM_GID:?set KVM_GID}
      NEKO_WEBRTC_UDPMUX: "59102"
      NEKO_WEBRTC_TCPMUX: "59102"
    ports: ["59102:59102/udp", "59102:59102/tcp"]
```

`KVM_GID` must not be required when the `desktop` profile is off. Compose interpolates `${KVM_GID:?…}` only for services that are active, but if the local Compose version errors anyway, use `${KVM_GID:-}` and rely on the entrypoint's `KVM_GID is required` check. Record which in a comment. Add `DESKTOP_SLOTS: ${DESKTOP_SLOTS:-}` to the `agent` env if Task P1.2 has not already.

In `compose.prod.yml`, add `x-desktop-prod`, copied from `x-slot-prod` with `image: mastertutor/desktop-slot:prod`, no `security_opt: apparmor=…` and the same logging block. Add `desktop-1` and `desktop-2` entries with Traefik labels copied from `browser-1`'s, with `browser-1` replaced by `desktop-1` (and so on) in every router and service name and in the `live_slot=` cookie matcher.

```ts
import { once } from "node:events";
import type { AddressInfo } from "node:net";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { createProxyControl } from "./control.ts";
import { EgressStateStore } from "./state.ts";

async function start() {
  const state = new EgressStateStore();
  const control = createProxyControl(state);
  control.server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1");
  await once(control.server, "listening");
  return { state, control, url: `http://127.0.0.1:${(control.server.address() as AddressInfo).port}` };
}

describe("proxy control", () => {
  it("applies validated state and keeps taint one-way", async () => {
    const { state, control, url } = await start();
    const put = (body: unknown) => fetch(`${url}/state`, { method: "PUT", body: JSON.stringify(body) });
    expect((await put({ allowedOrigins: ["https://a.example"], tainted: true })).status).toBe(204);
    expect((await put({ allowedOrigins: [], tainted: false })).status).toBe(204);
    expect(state.scope().tainted).toBe(true);
    expect((await put({ allowedOrigins: ["not an origin"], tainted: false })).status).toBe(400);
    control.server.close();
  });

  it("resets for a new lease", async () => {
    const { state, control, url } = await start();
    state.apply({ allowedOrigins: [], tainted: true });
    expect((await fetch(`${url}/reset`, { method: "POST" })).status).toBe(204);
    expect(state.scope().tainted).toBe(false);
    control.server.close();
  });

  it("streams notices as NDJSON to subscribers", async () => {
    const { control, url } = await start();
    const response = await fetch(`${url}/events`);
    const reader = response.body!.getReader();
    control.publish({ origin: "https://x.example", site: "x.example", decision: "denied" });
    const { value } = await reader.read();
    const event = JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(value).trim());
    expect(event).toMatchObject({ origin: "https://x.example", site: "x.example", decision: "denied" });
    expect(typeof event.at).toBe("number");
    await reader.cancel();
    control.server.close();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** In `desk.ts`:

```ts
import { Origin } from "./primitives.ts";

/** D53c §8.4: the proxy's per-lease state; taint is one-way within a lease. */
export const EgressState = z.strictObject({
  allowedOrigins: z.array(Origin).max(200),
  tainted: z.boolean(),
});
export type EgressState = z.infer<typeof EgressState>;
export const EGRESS_DECISIONS = ["allowed_first", "denied", "private_blocked"] as const;
export const EgressEvent = z.strictObject({
  at: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  origin: Origin,
  site: z.string().min(1).max(253),
  decision: z.enum(EGRESS_DECISIONS),
});
export type EgressEvent = z.infer<typeof EgressEvent>;
/** The proxy's loopback-only control port inside the desktop container. */
export const EGRESS_CONTROL_PORT = 3129;
```

`apps/desktop-host/src/egress/control.ts`:

```ts
import { createServer, type IncomingMessage, type Server, type ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import { EgressState } from "@mastertutor/contracts";
import type { EgressNotice, EgressStateStore } from "./state.ts";

const MAX_BODY = 65_536;

export async function readBody(req: IncomingMessage): Promise<string | null> {
  let size = 0;
  const chunks: Buffer[] = [];
  for await (const chunk of req as AsyncIterable<Buffer>) {
    size += chunk.length;
    if (size > MAX_BODY) return null;
    chunks.push(chunk);
  }
  return Buffer.concat(chunks).toString("utf8");
}

/** Loopback only (127.0.0.1:3129): the controller is its one client (spec §5.2). */
export function createProxyControl(state: EgressStateStore): { server: Server; publish(notice: EgressNotice): void } {
  const subscribers = new Set<ServerResponse>();
  const server = createServer(async (req, res) => {
    if (req.method === "PUT" && req.url === "/state") {
      const body = await readBody(req);
      let parsed;
      try {
        parsed = body === null ? null : EgressState.safeParse(JSON.parse(body));
      } catch {
        parsed = null;
      }
      if (!parsed?.success) return res.writeHead(400).end();
      state.apply(parsed.data);
      return res.writeHead(204).end();
    }
    if (req.method === "POST" && req.url === "/reset") {
      state.reset();
      return res.writeHead(204).end();
    }
    if (req.method === "GET" && req.url === "/events") {
      res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": "application/x-ndjson" });
      subscribers.add(res);
      req.once("close", () => subscribers.delete(res));
      return;
    }
    res.writeHead(404).end();
  });
  return {
    server,
    publish(notice) {
      const line = `${JSON.stringify({ at: Date.now(), ...notice })}\n`;
      for (const subscriber of subscribers) subscriber.write(line);
    },
  };
}
```

In `egress/main.ts`, create `const control = createProxyControl(state)`, listen on `127.0.0.1:${EGRESS_CONTROL_PORT}`, and call `control.publish(notice)` in `onNotice` beside the log line.

In `control-server.ts`, add the optional `egress` parameter and these routes before the 404:

```ts
      if (egress && req.method === "PUT" && req.url === "/egress") {
        const body = await readBody(req);
        let parsed;
        try {
          parsed = body === null ? null : EgressState.safeParse(JSON.parse(body));
        } catch {
          parsed = null;
        }
        if (!parsed?.success) return send(res, 400, { error: "invalid" });
        await egress.put(parsed.data);
        return res.writeHead(204).end();
      }
      if (egress && req.method === "GET" && req.url === "/egress/events") {
        res.writeHead(200, { "content-type": "application/x-ndjson" });
        const stream = await egress.events();
        for await (const chunk of stream) res.write(chunk);
        return res.end();
      }
```

`readBody` is imported from `./egress/control.ts`, so there is one body cap. In `main.ts`, build the `EgressPort` with `fetch("http://127.0.0.1:3129/state", { method: "PUT", body })` and `(await fetch("http://127.0.0.1:3129/events")).body!`. Pass `onRecycled: () => void fetch("http://127.0.0.1:3129/reset", { method: "POST" }).catch(() => log.error({}, "proxy reset failed"))` to the controller.

The proxy's reset must finish before the guest's first connection. The controller calls `onRecycled` before it launches the restored VM, so make `onRecycled` awaitable: change its type to `onRecycled?(): Promise<void>` and await it in `#restore`. Update the controller test's fake to match.

```ts
import { once } from "node:events";
import { createServer, type ServerResponse } from "node:http";
import type { AddressInfo } from "node:net";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { installTestTelemetry } from "@mastertutor/telemetry/testing";
import { openEgressChannel } from "./egress.ts";

describe("egress channel", () => {
  it("pushes state only when it changed, and turns denials into blocked navigations", async () => {
    const puts: string[] = [];
    let events!: ServerResponse;
    const server = createServer((req, res) => {
      if (req.url === "/egress" && req.method === "PUT") {
        let body = "";
        req.on("data", (c) => (body += c));
        req.on("end", () => { puts.push(body); res.writeHead(204).end(); });
      } else if (req.url === "/egress/events") {
        res.writeHead(200);
        events = res;
      }
    });
    server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1");
    await once(server, "listening");
    const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${(server.address() as AddressInfo).port}`;
    const telemetry = installTestTelemetry();
    const channel = openEgressChannel({ controlUrl: async (path) => `${base}${path}`, runId: "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001" });
    const state = { allowedOrigins: ["https://a.example"], tainted: false };
    await channel.sync(state);
    await channel.sync(state);
    await channel.sync({ ...state, tainted: true });
    expect(puts).toHaveLength(2);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(events).toBeDefined());
    events.write('{"at":1,"origin":"https://evil.example","site":"evil.example","decision":"denied"}\n');
    events.write('{"at":2,"origin":"https://cdn.example","site":"cdn.example","decision":"allowed_first"}\n');
    events.write("garbage\n");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(channel.drainDenied()).toEqual([{ url: "https://evil.example/", origin: "https://evil.example" }]));
    expect(channel.drainDenied()).toEqual([]);
    await vi.waitFor(() =>
      expect(telemetry.spans("mt.egress.decision").map((span) => span.attributes["mt.egress.decision"]).sort()).toEqual(["allowed_first", "denied"]),
    );
    channel.close();
    server.close();
  });
});
```

`installTestTelemetry` and `.spans(name)` stand for the helpers `@mastertutor/telemetry/testing` already provides (see `pool.test.ts`'s import). Use their real names.

`apps/agent/src/desk/desktop-browser.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { FakeLoopBrowser } from "../testing/fake-loop-browser.ts";
import { DesktopLoopBrowser } from "./desktop-browser.ts";
import { DesktopState } from "./desktop-state.ts";
import type { EgressChannel } from "./egress.ts";

function channel() {
  const synced: unknown[] = [];
  const denied = [{ url: "https://evil.example/", origin: "https://evil.example" }];
  const egress: EgressChannel = {
    sync: async (state) => void synced.push(state),
    drainDenied: () => denied.splice(0),
    close: () => undefined,
  };
  return { egress, synced };
}

describe("DesktopLoopBrowser", () => {
  it("syncs the run's scope and taint before each observation", async () => {
    const { egress, synced } = channel();
    const state = new DesktopState();
    const browser = new DesktopLoopBrowser(new FakeLoopBrowser(), { egress, state, run: () => ({ allowedOrigins: ["https://a.example"] }) });
    await browser.observe(new AbortController().signal);
    state.taint();
    await browser.observe(new AbortController().signal);
    expect(synced).toEqual([
      { allowedOrigins: ["https://a.example"], tainted: false },
      { allowedOrigins: ["https://a.example"], tainted: true },
    ]);
  });

  it("reports proxy denials beside the page's own blocked navigations", () => {
    const { egress } = channel();
    const inner = new FakeLoopBrowser();
    inner.blocked.push({ url: "https://other.example/x", origin: "https://other.example" });
    const browser = new DesktopLoopBrowser(inner, { egress, state: new DesktopState(), run: () => ({ allowedOrigins: [] }) });
    expect(browser.drainBlockedNavigations().map((b) => b.origin)).toEqual(["https://other.example", "https://evil.example"]);
  });
});
```

```ts
/**
 * D53c §8.3: per-lease desktop state. Taint is set before the first `shell` call or native-app
 * action executes, and never cleared; the VM recycle ends the lease and this object with it.
 */
export class DesktopState {
  #tainted = false;
  get tainted(): boolean {
    return this.#tainted;
  }
  taint(): void {
    this.#tainted = true;
  }
}
```

`apps/agent/src/desk/egress.ts`:

```ts
import { EgressEvent, type EgressState } from "@mastertutor/contracts";
import { ATTR, SPAN } from "@mastertutor/contracts/telemetry";
import { instrument } from "@mastertutor/telemetry/instrument";
import type { BlockedNavigation } from "../browser/network-policy.ts";

export interface EgressChannel {
  /** Pushes the run's scope and taint to the proxy, only when they changed. */
  sync(state: EgressState): Promise<void>;
  /** Origins the proxy refused since the last call: each becomes a new_origin approval. */
  drainDenied(): BlockedNavigation[];
  close(): void;
}

/** One per desktop lease: a pushed state and one long-lived NDJSON event stream (no polling). */
export function openEgressChannel(options: {
  controlUrl(path: string): Promise<string>;
  runId: string;
  fetch?: typeof fetch;
  retryMs?: number;
}): EgressChannel {
  const http = options.fetch ?? fetch;
  const abort = new AbortController();
  const denied: BlockedNavigation[] = [];
  let last = "";

  const onEvent = (event: EgressEvent) => {
    void instrument(
      SPAN.egressDecision,
      { [ATTR.runId]: options.runId, [ATTR.egressDecision]: event.decision, [ATTR.egressSite]: event.site },
      async () => undefined,
    );
    if (event.decision === "denied" && denied.length < 50)
      denied.push({ url: `${event.origin}/`, origin: event.origin });
  };

  const listen = async () => {
    while (!abort.signal.aborted) {
      try {
        const response = await http(await options.controlUrl("/egress/events"), { signal: abort.signal });
        let buffered = "";
        for await (const chunk of response.body as AsyncIterable<Uint8Array>) {
          buffered += new TextDecoder().decode(chunk);
          if (buffered.length > 65_536) break;
          for (let end = buffered.indexOf("\n"); end >= 0; end = buffered.indexOf("\n")) {
            const line = buffered.slice(0, end);
            buffered = buffered.slice(end + 1);
            try {
              const parsed = EgressEvent.safeParse(JSON.parse(line));
              if (parsed.success) onEvent(parsed.data);
            } catch {
              // An unparsable line from the slot is dropped; the stream goes on.
            }
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // The stream ended or the slot restarted: reconnect below unless closed.
      }
      if (!abort.signal.aborted) await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, options.retryMs ?? 1_000));
    }
  };
  void listen();

  return {
    async sync(state) {
      const body = JSON.stringify(state);
      if (body === last) return;
      const response = await http(await options.controlUrl("/egress"), { method: "PUT", body, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(2_000) });
      if (response.status !== 204) throw new Error(`egress sync failed: ${response.status}`);
      last = body;
    },
    drainDenied: () => denied.splice(0),
    close: () => abort.abort(),
  };
}
```

`apps/agent/src/desk/desktop-browser.ts` is a decorator. Every `LoopBrowser` member delegates to `inner`, except `observe` and `drainBlockedNavigations`. Task P3.8 adds `targetFor` and `runComputer`.

```ts
import type { ApprovalRequest, ComputerAction, FunctionToolName, ScrollPosition } from "@mastertutor/contracts";
import type { BlockedNavigation } from "../browser/network-policy.ts";
import type { TargetDescription } from "../browser/page-helpers.ts";
import type { BrowserStorageState } from "../browser/storage-state.ts";
import type { LoopBrowser, Observation } from "../loop/loop-browser.ts";
import type { ActionGate } from "../tools/computer.ts";
import type { CallApproval, StepWriter } from "../tools/types.ts";
import type { DesktopState } from "./desktop-state.ts";
import type { EgressChannel } from "./egress.ts";

export interface DesktopBrowserDeps {
  egress: EgressChannel;
  state: DesktopState;
  run(): { allowedOrigins: readonly string[] };
}

/** D53c: a desktop lease's LoopBrowser: the slot's browser, plus the VM boundary's egress and taint. */
export class DesktopLoopBrowser implements LoopBrowser {
  readonly #inner: LoopBrowser;
  readonly #deps: DesktopBrowserDeps;

  constructor(inner: LoopBrowser, deps: DesktopBrowserDeps) {
    this.#inner = inner;
    this.#deps = deps;
  }

  get signInFlow() {
    return this.#inner.signInFlow;
  }

  async observe(signal: AbortSignal): Promise<Observation> {
    await this.#deps.egress.sync({
      allowedOrigins: [...this.#deps.run().allowedOrigins],
      tainted: this.#deps.state.tainted,
    });
    return this.#inner.observe(signal);
  }

  drainBlockedNavigations(): BlockedNavigation[] {
    return [...this.#inner.drainBlockedNavigations(), ...this.#deps.egress.drainDenied()];
  }

  targetFor(action: ComputerAction, previous: TargetDescription | null, signal: AbortSignal) {
    return this.#inner.targetFor(action, previous, signal);
  }
  runComputer(actions: readonly ComputerAction[], signal: AbortSignal, gate: ActionGate) {
    return this.#inner.runComputer(actions, signal, gate);
  }
  functionApproval(name: FunctionToolName, args: unknown, signal: AbortSignal): Promise<ApprovalRequest | null> {
    return this.#inner.functionApproval(name, args, signal);
  }
  runFunction(name: FunctionToolName, args: unknown, signal: AbortSignal, approval: CallApproval | null, step: StepWriter) {
    return this.#inner.runFunction(name, args, signal, approval, step);
  }
  navigate(url: string, signal: AbortSignal) {
    return this.#inner.navigate(url, signal);
  }
  restoreView(view: { scroll: ScrollPosition | null; videoTime: number | null }) {
    return this.#inner.restoreView(view);
  }
  drainBlockedDownloads() {
    return this.#inner.drainBlockedDownloads();
  }
  allowDownload(card: { url: string; filename: string | null; approvedBy: string }) {
    return this.#inner.allowDownload(card);
  }
  collectStorage() {
    return this.#inner.collectStorage();
  }
  applyStorage(state: BrowserStorageState) {
    return this.#inner.applyStorage(state);
  }
}
```

```ts
describe.skipIf(!process.env.KVM_GID)("desktop boundary (D53c §5.3, §8)", () => {
  it("refuses metadata, private and loopback targets at the proxy, and reports them", async () => {
    for (const target of ["169.254.169.254:80", "10.0.0.1:443", "127.0.0.1:9222", "[::1]:443", "2130706433:443"])
      expect(await connectThroughProxy(target)).toMatch(/^HTTP\/1\.1 (403|400)/);
    const events = await nextEgressEvents(5);
    expect(events.every((e: { decision: string }) => e.decision === "private_blocked" || e.decision === "denied")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("denies an out-of-scope site after taint, and allows it again only once approved", async () => {
    await putEgress({ allowedOrigins: [SITE], tainted: true });
    expect(await connectThroughProxy(`other.fixtures-isolated.test:80`, "absolute")).toMatch(/^HTTP\/1\.1 403/);
    await putEgress({ allowedOrigins: [SITE, OTHER], tainted: true });
    expect(await connectThroughProxy(`other.fixtures-isolated.test:80`, "absolute")).toMatch(/^HTTP\/1\.1 200/);
  });

  it("keeps the guest's Chromium inside the proxy: a page cannot reach a private address", async () => {
    const page = await openGuestPage(SITE);
    const reached = await page.evaluate(() => fetch("http://169.254.169.254/latest/meta-data/").then(() => true, () => false));
    expect(reached).toBe(false);
  });

  it("admits the agent-only ports from the agent address only", async () => {
    expect(await tcpOpen(desktopIpOnCdp, 9226, { from: "a container that is not the agent" })).toBe(false);
  });

  it("has no IPv6 and no UDP way out of the guest", async () => {
    const rules = await dockerExec("desktop-1", ["nft", "list", "ruleset"]);
    expect(rules).toMatch(/table ip6 mt6[\s\S]*policy drop/);
    expect(rules).toMatch(/iifname "tap0" drop/);
  });
});
```

`connectThroughProxy` runs `node -e` inside the desktop container through `docker exec`. That reaches `192.168.127.1:3128` over loopback, which is the path the guest's packets take after the tap. `nextEgressEvents` reads `GET /egress/events` on the published control port. `openGuestPage` attaches Playwright to the guest's Chromium through the published CDP port. `tcpOpen … from` runs a throwaway `alpine` container (labelled `mastertutor.ci=1`) on the behaviour network. `SITE` and `OTHER` come from `tests/behaviour/constants.ts`. Write these helpers at the top of the file. They are fixtures, not product code.

```ts
import { DeskPath } from "./primitives.ts";

export const TOOL_PROFILE_TOOLS = {
  browser_use: ["computer", "read_page", "capture", "fill_credential", "use_passkey", "video", "annotate"],
  computer_use: ["computer", "fill_credential", "use_passkey"],
  /** D53c: the desktop; no vault tools (decision a), and the only profile with shell (decision b). */
  desktop_use: ["computer", "read_page", "capture", "video", "annotate", "shell"],

export const ShellArgs = z.object({
  command: z.string().min(1).max(4_000),
  cwd: DeskPath.nullable(),
  timeoutSec: z.number().int().min(1).max(120).nullable(),
});
export type ShellArgs = z.infer<typeof ShellArgs>;
export const ShellResult = z.object({
  exitCode: z.number().int().min(-1).max(255),
  stdout: z.string().max(32_000),
  stderr: z.string().max(8_000),

});
```

`browser_use` was `TOOL_NAMES`. It is now the explicit list of the same seven tools, so adding `shell` to `TOOL_NAMES` changes nothing for browser runs.

`CaptureArgs`:

```ts
export const CaptureArgs = z
  .object({
    scope: z.enum(CAPTURE_SCOPES),
    selector: z.string().min(1).max(2_000).nullable(),
    kind: z.enum(["web", "pdf", "file"]).nullable(),
    /** kind "file" only (D53c §10.4): a document inside the desktop, captured from its bytes. */
    path: DeskPath.nullable(),
  })
  .superRefine((args, ctx) => {
    if (args.scope === "element" && args.selector === null)
      ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["selector"], message: "element scope needs a selector" });
    if ((args.kind === "file") !== (args.path !== null))
      ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["path"], message: "a file capture needs a path, and only a file capture" });
  });
```

`desk.ts`: the full method list, params and results:

```ts
export const DESK_METHODS = ["hello", "fileGet", "screenshot", "input", "windows", "hitTest", "exec", "filePut", "freeze"] as const;
const Name = z.string().max(500);
export const DeskWindow = z.strictObject({
  id: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  app: z.string().max(64),
  title: Name,
  x: z.number().int(), y: z.number().int(),
  w: z.number().int().min(0).max(8_192), h: z.number().int().min(0).max(8_192),
  focused: z.boolean(),
});
export type DeskWindow = z.infer<typeof DeskWindow>;
export const DeskHit = z.strictObject({ app: z.string().max(64), role: z.string().max(64), name: Name, editable: z.boolean() });
export type DeskHit = z.infer<typeof DeskHit>;

export const DESK_PARAMS = {
  hello: z.strictObject({}),
  fileGet: z.strictObject({ path: DeskPath, maxBytes: Bytes.min(1) }),
  screenshot: z.strictObject({}),
  input: z.strictObject({ action: ComputerAction }),
  windows: z.strictObject({}),
  hitTest: z.strictObject({ x: z.number().int().min(0).max(VIEWPORT.width - 1), y: z.number().int().min(0).max(VIEWPORT.height - 1) }),
  exec: z.strictObject({
    command: z.string().min(1).max(4_000),
    cwd: DeskPath.nullable(),
    timeoutMs: z.number().int().min(1).max(120_000),
    maxBytes: z.number().int().min(1).max(65_536),
  }),
  filePut: z.strictObject({ path: DeskPath }),
  freeze: z.strictObject({ on: z.boolean() }),
} as const satisfies Record<DeskMethod, z.ZodType>;

export const DESK_RESULTS = {
  hello: z.strictObject({ methods: z.array(z.string().max(32)).max(32), version: z.string().max(32) }),
  fileGet: z.strictObject({ size: Bytes }),
  /** Raw BGRX pixels follow (width × height × 4 bytes); the agent encodes the PNG. */
  screenshot: z.strictObject({ width: z.literal(VIEWPORT.width), height: z.literal(VIEWPORT.height) }),
  input: z.strictObject({ ok: z.literal(true) }),
  windows: z.strictObject({ windows: z.array(DeskWindow).max(64) }),
  hitTest: z.strictObject({ hit: DeskHit.nullable() }),
  exec: z.strictObject({
    exitCode: z.number().int().min(-1).max(255),
    stdout: z.string().max(65_536),
    stderr: z.string().max(65_536),
    truncated: z.boolean(),
    timedOut: z.boolean(),
    ms: z.number().int().nonnegative(),
  }),
  filePut: z.strictObject({ size: Bytes }),
  freeze: z.strictObject({ ok: z.literal(true) }),
} as const satisfies Record<DeskMethod, z.ZodType>;
```

```python
class Input(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_maps_computer_actions_to_xdotool_argv(self):
        self.assertEqual(deskd.xdotool_argv({"type": "click", "x": 10, "y": 20, "button": "right"}),
                         [["mousemove", "--sync", "10", "20", "click", "3"]])
        self.assertEqual(deskd.xdotool_argv({"type": "double_click", "x": 1, "y": 2}),
                         [["mousemove", "--sync", "1", "2", "click", "--repeat", "2", "1"]])
        self.assertEqual(deskd.xdotool_argv({"type": "keypress", "keys": ["CTRL", "c"]}), [["key", "--", "ctrl+c"]])
        self.assertEqual(deskd.xdotool_argv({"type": "keypress", "keys": ["ENTER"]}), [["key", "--", "Return"]])
        self.assertEqual(deskd.xdotool_argv({"type": "type", "text": "-rf /"}), [["type", "--delay", "8", "--", "-rf /"]])
        self.assertEqual(deskd.xdotool_argv({"type": "scroll", "x": 5, "y": 5, "scroll_x": 0, "scroll_y": 250}),
                         [["mousemove", "--sync", "5", "5"], ["click", "--repeat", "3", "5"]])
        self.assertEqual(deskd.xdotool_argv({"type": "wait"}), [])

    def test_refuses_unknown_keys_and_out_of_range_points(self):
        with self.assertRaises(deskd.DeskError):
            deskd.xdotool_argv({"type": "keypress", "keys": ["HYPER_LAUNCH"]})
        with self.assertRaises(deskd.DeskError):
            deskd.xdotool_argv({"type": "click", "x": 5000, "y": 1, "button": "left"})


class Exec(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        self.home = tempfile.mkdtemp()
        deskd.HOME = self.home + "/"
        deskd.EXEC_CGROUP = None  # no cgroup in the build container

    def test_runs_captures_and_caps_output(self):
        result, _ = deskd.exec_({"command": "printf abc; printf err >&2; exit 3", "cwd": None, "timeoutMs": 5000, "maxBytes": 100}, b"")
        self.assertEqual((result["exitCode"], result["stdout"], result["stderr"], result["truncated"], result["timedOut"]),
                         (3, "abc", "err", False, False))
        big, _ = deskd.exec_({"command": "head -c 5000 /dev/zero | tr '\\0' a", "cwd": None, "timeoutMs": 5000, "maxBytes": 100}, b"")
        self.assertEqual((len(big["stdout"]), big["truncated"]), (100, True))

    def test_kills_the_whole_group_on_timeout(self):
        result, _ = deskd.exec_({"command": "sleep 30 & sleep 30", "cwd": None, "timeoutMs": 200, "maxBytes": 100}, b"")
        self.assertTrue(result["timedOut"])
        self.assertEqual(result["exitCode"], -1)


class Files(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_file_put_writes_below_home_and_never_through_a_symlink(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as home:
            deskd.HOME = home + "/"
            target = os.path.join(home, "in", "a.txt")
            result, _ = deskd.file_put({"path": target}, b"hello")
            self.assertEqual(result, {"size": 5})
            os.symlink("/tmp/outside", os.path.join(home, "evil"))
            with self.assertRaises(deskd.DeskError):
                deskd.file_put({"path": os.path.join(home, "evil")}, b"x")
```
