- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the methods** in `deskd.py`. Set `METHODS` to the nine names in contract order, then add:

```python
import select
import signal
import subprocess
import time

WIDTH, HEIGHT = 1280, 800
EXEC_CGROUP = "/sys/fs/cgroup/agent-exec"
BUTTONS = {"left": "1", "wheel": "2", "right": "3", "back": "8", "forward": "9"}
KEYS = {
    "ENTER": "Return", "RETURN": "Return", "TAB": "Tab", "ESC": "Escape", "ESCAPE": "Escape",
    "BACKSPACE": "BackSpace", "DELETE": "Delete", "SPACE": "space", "CTRL": "ctrl", "CONTROL": "ctrl",
    "ALT": "alt", "SHIFT": "shift", "META": "super", "CMD": "super", "SUPER": "super",
    "UP": "Up", "DOWN": "Down", "LEFT": "Left", "RIGHT": "Right", "ARROWUP": "Up", "ARROWDOWN": "Down",
    "ARROWLEFT": "Left", "ARROWRIGHT": "Right", "HOME": "Home", "END": "End", "PAGEUP": "Prior",
    "PAGEDOWN": "Next", "INSERT": "Insert",
}
KEYS.update({"F%d" % n: "F%d" % n for n in range(1, 13)})


def _point(action):
    x, y = action.get("x"), action.get("y")
    if not all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) for v in (x, y)) or not (0 <= x < WIDTH and 0 <= y < HEIGHT):
        raise DeskError("bad_params", "point out of range")
    return [str(x), str(y)]


def _key(name):
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name:
        raise DeskError("bad_params", "invalid key")
    if len(name) == 1 and name.isprintable():
        return name.lower() if name.isalpha() else name
    mapped = KEYS.get(name.upper())
    if mapped is None:
        raise DeskError("bad_params", "unknown key")
    return mapped


def xdotool_argv(action):
    kind = action.get("type")
    if kind == "click":
        return [["mousemove", "--sync", *_point(action), "click", BUTTONS.get(action.get("button", "left"), "1")]]
    if kind == "double_click":
        return [["mousemove", "--sync", *_point(action), "click", "--repeat", "2", "1"]]
    if kind == "move":
        return [["mousemove", "--sync", *_point(action)]]
    if kind == "drag":
        path = action.get("path") or []
        if len(path) < 2:
            raise DeskError("bad_params", "drag needs two points")
        steps = [["mousemove", "--sync", *_point(path[0]), "mousedown", "1"]]
        steps += [["mousemove", "--sync", *_point(p)] for p in path[1:]]
        return steps + [["mouseup", "1"]]
    if kind == "scroll":
        steps = [["mousemove", "--sync", *_point(action)]]
        for delta, minus, plus in ((action.get("scroll_y", 0), "4", "5"), (action.get("scroll_x", 0), "6", "7")):
            if delta:
                steps.append(["click", "--repeat", str(min(50, -(-abs(delta) // 100))), plus if delta > 0 else minus])
        return steps
    if kind == "keypress":
        keys = action.get("keys") or []
        return [["key", "--", "+".join(_key(k) for k in keys)]]
    if kind == "type":
        return [["type", "--delay", "8", "--", str(action.get("text", ""))[:5000]]]
    if kind in ("wait", "screenshot"):
        return []
    raise DeskError("bad_params", "unknown action")


def input_(params, _payload):
    for argv in xdotool_argv(params.get("action") or {}):
        subprocess.run(["xdotool", *argv], check=True, timeout=10, env={"DISPLAY": ":99.0"})
    return {"ok": True}, b""


def screenshot(_params, _payload):
    from Xlib import X, display
    screen = display.Display(":99.0").screen()
    raw = screen.root.get_image(0, 0, WIDTH, HEIGHT, X.ZPixmap, 0xFFFFFFFF).data
    if len(raw) != WIDTH * HEIGHT * 4:
        raise DeskError("bad_screen", "unexpected screen size")
    return {"width": WIDTH, "height": HEIGHT}, bytes(raw)


def windows(_params, _payload):
    from Xlib import display
    d = display.Display(":99.0")
    root = d.screen().root
    atom = d.intern_atom
    active = root.get_full_property(atom("_NET_ACTIVE_WINDOW"), 0)
    active_id = active.value[0] if active else 0
    stacking = root.get_full_property(atom("_NET_CLIENT_LIST_STACKING"), 0)
    out = []
    for wid in (stacking.value if stacking else [])[-64:]:
        window = d.create_resource_object("window", wid)
        try:
            geometry = window.get_geometry()
            origin = window.translate_coords(root, 0, 0)
            name = window.get_full_property(atom("_NET_WM_NAME"), 0)
            klass = window.get_wm_class() or ("", "")
        except Exception:
            continue
        title = name.value.decode("utf-8", "replace") if name else ""
        out.append({"id": int(wid), "app": str(klass[1])[:64].lower(), "title": title[:500],
                    "x": -origin.x, "y": -origin.y, "w": geometry.width, "h": geometry.height,
                    "focused": wid == active_id})
    return {"windows": out}, b""


def hit_test(params, _payload):
    import pyatspi
    x, y = (int(v) for v in _point(params))
    signal.alarm(2)
    try:
        for app in pyatspi.Registry.getDesktop(0):
            for frame in app or []:
                try:
                    component = frame.queryComponent()
                except NotImplementedError:
                    continue
                if not component.contains(x, y, pyatspi.DESKTOP_COORDS) or not frame.getState().contains(pyatspi.STATE_ACTIVE):
                    continue
                node = frame
                while True:
                    child = node.queryComponent().getAccessibleAtPoint(x, y, pyatspi.DESKTOP_COORDS)
                    if child is None or child == node:
                        break
                    node = child
                return {"hit": {"app": (app.name or "")[:64].lower(), "role": node.getRoleName()[:64],
                                "name": (node.name or "")[:500],
                                "editable": node.getState().contains(pyatspi.STATE_EDITABLE)}}, b""
        return {"hit": None}, b""
    finally:
        signal.alarm(0)


def _join_cgroup():
    if EXEC_CGROUP:
        with open(os.path.join(EXEC_CGROUP, "cgroup.procs"), "w") as procs:
            procs.write(str(os.getpid()))


def exec_(params, _payload):
    command, cwd = params.get("command"), params.get("cwd")
    if not isinstance(command, str) or not 1 <= len(command) <= 4000:
        raise DeskError("bad_params", "invalid command")
    cwd = safe_path(cwd) if cwd is not None else HOME
    timeout = _limit(params, "timeoutMs") / 1000
    limit = params.get("maxBytes")
    if not isinstance(limit, int) or not 1 <= limit <= 65536:
        raise DeskError("bad_params", "invalid maxBytes")
    started = time.monotonic()
    process = subprocess.Popen(["bash", "-lc", command], cwd=cwd, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE,
                               stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, start_new_session=True, preexec_fn=_join_cgroup)
    buffers = {process.stdout: bytearray(), process.stderr: bytearray()}
    truncated = timed_out = False
    open_streams = set(buffers)
    while open_streams:
        remaining = timeout - (time.monotonic() - started)
        if remaining <= 0:
            timed_out = True
            break
        ready, _, _ = select.select(list(open_streams), [], [], remaining)
        for stream in ready:
            chunk = os.read(stream.fileno(), 65536)
            if not chunk:
                open_streams.discard(stream)
                continue
            room = limit - len(buffers[stream])
            buffers[stream] += chunk[:max(0, room)]
            truncated = truncated or len(chunk) > room
    if timed_out:
        os.killpg(process.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
    code = process.wait()
    return {"exitCode": -1 if timed_out else max(-1, min(255, code)),
            "stdout": buffers[process.stdout].decode("utf-8", "replace"),
            "stderr": buffers[process.stderr].decode("utf-8", "replace"),
            "truncated": truncated, "timedOut": timed_out,
            "ms": int((time.monotonic() - started) * 1000)}, b""


def file_put(params, payload):
    path = safe_path(params.get("path"))
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    try:
        fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC | os.O_NOFOLLOW, 0o644)
    except OSError:
        raise DeskError("bad_path", "cannot write there")
    with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as handle:
        handle.write(payload)
    return {"size": len(payload)}, b""


def freeze(params, _payload):
    on = params.get("on")
    if not isinstance(on, bool):
        raise DeskError("bad_params", "invalid on")
    with open(os.path.join(EXEC_CGROUP, "cgroup.freeze"), "w") as handle:
        handle.write("1" if on else "0")
    return {"ok": True}, b""


HANDLERS = {"hello": hello, "fileGet": file_get, "screenshot": screenshot, "input": input_, "windows": windows,
            "hitTest": hit_test, "exec": exec_, "filePut": file_put, "freeze": freeze}
```

Raw pixels go to the agent, which encodes the PNG (Task P3.6). `safe_path` already rejects a symlink that resolves outside `HOME`, and `O_NOFOLLOW` covers a symlink created in between. So that `hit_test`'s alarm raises a `DeskError`, add this at module level:

```python
def _alarm(_signum, _frame):
    raise DeskError("timeout", "accessibility query timed out")


signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, _alarm)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** Generalise `call` to take an optional outgoing payload: `encodeFrame({ id, method, params, bytes: payload.length }, payload)`. Wrap each public method in `instrument(SPAN.deskAction, { [ATTR.deskMethod]: method }, async (span) => …)`. It sets `deskOutcome` to `ok`, `error` (DeskRefused) or `timeout` (DeskUnavailable `timeout`), and `deskBytes` to the payload length. Then:

```ts
    async screenshot(signal) {
      const { payload } = await call("screenshot", {}, signal);
      if (payload.length !== VIEWPORT.width * VIEWPORT.height * 4) throw new DeskUnavailable("bad_screen");
      return sharp(payload, { raw: { width: VIEWPORT.width, height: VIEWPORT.height, channels: 4 } })
        .removeAlpha()
        .recomb([[0, 0, 1], [0, 1, 0], [1, 0, 0]]) // BGRX → RGB
        .png({ compressionLevel: 1 })
        .toBuffer();
    },
    async exec(params, signal) {
      return instrument(SPAN.deskExec, {}, async (span) => {
        const { result } = await call("exec", params, signal);
        const r = result as DeskExecResult;
        span.set({
          [ATTR.deskExitCode]: r.exitCode,
          [ATTR.deskOutputBytes]: Buffer.byteLength(r.stdout) + Buffer.byteLength(r.stderr),
          [ATTR.deskTimedOut]: r.timedOut,
          [ATTR.deskMs]: r.ms,
        });
        return r;
      });
    },
```

`input`, `windows`, `hitTest`, `filePut` and `freeze` call `call(method, params, signal)` (and send the payload for `filePut`), returning the validated result. `DeskExecResult` is `z.infer<(typeof DESK_RESULTS)["exec"]>`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `lease.ts`:

```ts
import type { DeskClient } from "./client.ts";
import type { DesktopState } from "./desktop-state.ts";
import type { EgressChannel } from "./egress.ts";

export interface DesktopLease {
  readonly desk: DeskClient;
  readonly tainted: boolean;
  taint(): Promise<void>;
}

/** D53c §8.3: the proxy must know about taint before any guest code runs, so taint() awaits the push. */
export function createDesktopLease(deps: {
  desk: DeskClient;
  state: DesktopState;
  egress: EgressChannel;
  run(): { allowedOrigins: readonly string[] };
}): DesktopLease {
  return {
    desk: deps.desk,
    get tainted() {
      return deps.state.tainted;
    },
    async taint() {
      deps.state.taint();
      await deps.egress.sync({ allowedOrigins: [...deps.run().allowedOrigins], tainted: true });
    },
  };
}
```

`vault-off.ts`:

```ts
import type { RunHooks } from "../loop/hooks.ts";

/**
 * Decision (a), D53c §12.1: a desktop VM can read anything its browser holds, so desktop runs load
 * no sealed sessions and seal nothing back. Credential tools are absent from desktop_use already.
 */
export function withoutVaultInDesktops(hooks: RunHooks): RunHooks {
  return {
    ...hooks,
    sessionStore: {
      load: (run) => (run.environment === "desktop" ? Promise.resolve(null) : hooks.sessionStore.load(run)),
      save: (tx, run, collected) =>
        run.environment === "desktop" ? Promise.resolve() : hooks.sessionStore.save(tx, run, collected),
    },
  };
}
```

In `main.ts`, wrap: `hooks: withoutVaultInDesktops(composeRunHooks(vaultHooks(vault), liveHooks, libraryHooks(library)))`. Add `environment` to `RunSnapshot` in `run-state.ts`, filled from the run row.

`types.ts`: add `desktop: DesktopLease | null;` to `ToolContext` and `ApprovalContext`, with the doc "Desktop leases only (D53c): deskd and taint. Null for browser slots." The type import is from `../desk/lease.ts`. That is `tools/` importing a type from `desk/`, so add `desk/lease.ts` as an allowed type-only exception to the architecture rule from Task P1.14, and assert the import is `import type`.

```ts
import { entersCommand, inChromium, nativeTarget, toPage, topWindowAt } from "./desktop-computer.ts";

const win = (over: Partial<{ id: number; app: string; x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number; focused: boolean }>) => ({
  id: 1, app: "chromium", title: "t", x: 0, y: 0, w: 1280, h: 800, focused: false, ...over,
});
const content = { x: 0, y: 80, w: 1280, h: 720 };

describe("rule 1: the Chromium region", () => {
  it("is Chromium's page area while Chromium is top-most at the point", () => {
    expect(inChromium({ type: "click", x: 100, y: 200, button: "left" }, [win({})], content)).toBe(true);
    expect(inChromium({ type: "click", x: 100, y: 40, button: "left" }, [win({})], content)).toBe(false); // tab strip
    const terminal = win({ id: 2, app: "xfce4-terminal", x: 50, y: 150, w: 400, h: 300 });
    expect(inChromium({ type: "click", x: 100, y: 200, button: "left" }, [win({}), terminal], content)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("follows keyboard focus for keypress and type", () => {
    expect(inChromium({ type: "type", text: "x" }, [win({ focused: true })], content)).toBe(true);
    expect(inChromium({ type: "type", text: "x" }, [win({}), win({ id: 2, app: "mousepad", focused: true })], content)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("translates desktop coordinates into page coordinates, paths included", () => {
    expect(toPage({ type: "click", x: 100, y: 200, button: "left" }, content)).toEqual({ type: "click", x: 100, y: 120, button: "left" });
    expect(toPage({ type: "drag", path: [{ x: 10, y: 90 }, { x: 20, y: 100 }] }, content)).toEqual({ type: "drag", path: [{ x: 10, y: 10 }, { x: 20, y: 20 }] });
  });

  it("picks the top-most window at a point (stacking order, top last)", () => {
    const a = win({ id: 1 });
    const b = win({ id: 2, app: "pcmanfm", x: 0, y: 0, w: 200, h: 200 });
    expect(topWindowAt([a, b], { x: 10, y: 10 })?.id).toBe(2);
    expect(topWindowAt([a, b], { x: 500, y: 500 })?.id).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("rule 2: native targets", () => {
  it("labels by app and accessible name, and marks unnamed controls once tainted", () => {
    const named = nativeTarget({ app: "libreoffice", role: "push button", name: "Send", editable: false }, null, false);
    expect(named.label).toBe("libreoffice: Send");
    expect(named.native).toEqual({ app: "libreoffice", role: "push button", named: true, tainted: false });
    expect(nativeTarget(null, win({ app: "pcmanfm" }), true).native).toEqual({ app: "pcmanfm", role: "unknown", named: false, tainted: true });
    expect(nativeTarget(null, win({ app: "pcmanfm" }), true).label).toBe("pcmanfm: unnamed control");
  });

  it("recognises Enter into a terminal, typed or pressed", () => {
    const terminal = nativeTarget({ app: "xfce4-terminal", role: "terminal", name: "", editable: true }, null, false);
    expect(entersCommand({ type: "keypress", keys: ["ENTER"] }, terminal)).toBe(true);
    expect(entersCommand({ type: "type", text: "rm -rf ~\n" }, terminal)).toBe(true);
    expect(entersCommand({ type: "type", text: "ls" }, terminal)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `desktop-computer.ts`:

```ts
import type { ComputerAction, DeskHit, DeskWindow } from "@mastertutor/contracts";
import type { TargetDescription } from "../browser/page-helpers.ts";
import { normalizeCombo } from "../tools/keys.ts";

export interface ContentBox {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  w: number;
  h: number;
}

export const TERMINAL_ENTER_REFUSAL =
  "Refused: commands are not run by typing into a terminal. Use the shell tool, which shows the command for approval.";

export function pointOf(action: ComputerAction): { x: number; y: number } | null {
  switch (action.type) {
    case "click":
    case "double_click":
    case "move":
    case "scroll":
      return { x: action.x, y: action.y };
    case "drag":
      return action.path[0] ?? null;
    default:
      return null;
  }
}

const contains = (box: { x: number; y: number; w: number; h: number }, p: { x: number; y: number }) =>
  p.x >= box.x && p.y >= box.y && p.x < box.x + box.w && p.y < box.y + box.h;

/** EWMH stacking order lists the top-most window last. */
export function topWindowAt(windows: readonly DeskWindow[], point: { x: number; y: number }): DeskWindow | null {
  for (let i = windows.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) if (contains(windows[i]!, point)) return windows[i]!;
  return null;
}

const isChromium = (window: DeskWindow | null | undefined) => !!window && window.app.includes("chromium");

/** Rule 1: inside Chromium's page area with Chromium top-most there (or focused, for keys). */
export function inChromium(action: ComputerAction, windows: readonly DeskWindow[], content: ContentBox | null): boolean {
  if (!content) return false;
  const point = pointOf(action);
  if (!point) return isChromium(windows.find((w) => w.focused));
  const points = action.type === "drag" ? action.path : [point];
  return points.every((p) => contains(content, p) && isChromium(topWindowAt(windows, p)));
}

export function toPage(action: ComputerAction, content: ContentBox): ComputerAction {
  const shift = (p: { x: number; y: number }) => ({ x: p.x - content.x, y: p.y - content.y });
  if (action.type === "drag") return { ...action, path: action.path.map(shift) };
  const point = pointOf(action);
  return point ? ({ ...action, ...shift(point) } as ComputerAction) : action;
}

/** Rule 2: what an action outside the Chromium region acts on, as the approval classifier sees it. */
export function nativeTarget(hit: DeskHit | null, window: DeskWindow | null, tainted: boolean): TargetDescription {
  const app = hit?.app || window?.app || "desktop";
  const name = hit?.name.trim() ?? "";
  const role = hit?.role ?? "unknown";
  return {
    label: `${app}: ${name || "unnamed control"}`,
    tag: role,
    path: `desktop:${app}:${role}:${name}`,
    context: `desktop:${app}`,
    isFormSubmit: false,
    formKind: null,
    isSecretField: false,
    editable: hit?.editable ?? false,
    interactive: true,
    native: { app, role, named: name.length > 0, tainted },
  };
}

export function entersCommand(action: ComputerAction, target: TargetDescription): boolean {
  if (target.native?.role !== "terminal") return false;
  if (action.type === "type") return /[\r\n]/.test(action.text);
  return action.type === "keypress" && normalizeCombo(action.keys).split("+").at(-1) === "ENTER";
}
```

In `page-helpers.ts`, add to `TargetDescription`:

```ts
  /** D53c: a native desktop control (outside Chromium's page), from AT-SPI; the guest is untrusted. */
  native?: { app: string; role: string; named: boolean; tainted: boolean };
```

In `policy.ts` `needsApproval`, after `if (!target) return null;`:

```ts
  // D53c §11.2 rule 2: native controls. Risky names ask; once the run is tainted, unnamed ones ask too.
  if (target.native) {
    const combo = action.type === "keypress" ? normalizeCombo(action.keys).split("+") : [];
    const acts =
      action.type === "click" || action.type === "double_click" || action.type === "type" ||
      combo.at(-1) === "ENTER" || combo.at(-1) === "SPACE";
    if (!acts) return null;
    if (isRiskyLabel(target.label) || (!target.native.named && target.native.tainted))
      return { kind: "risky_click", label: target.label, action };
    return null;
  }
```

In `desktop-browser.ts`, extend the deps with `session: BrowserSession`, `lease: DesktopLease` and `profile(): ToolProfile`. When `profile() === "desktop_use"`:
- **`observe`** syncs egress, takes `inner.observe(signal)` for url, title, origin and the other page facts, then replaces `screenshot` with `{ png: await lease.desk.screenshot(signal), width: 1280, height: 800, scale: 1, masked: 0, dropped: false, withheld: null }` and `phash` with `perceptualHash(png)`. It sets `session.lastScale = 1` (desktop pixels are CSS pixels at DPR 1). It refreshes `#content`: CDP `Browser.getWindowForTarget` bounds, plus `Page.getLayoutMetrics().cssVisualViewport` client width and height, giving `{ x: left, y: top + height − vh, w: vw, h: vh }`.
- **`targetFor`**: if `inChromium(action, windows, content)`, return `inner.targetFor(toPage(action, content), previous, signal)`. Otherwise `nativeTarget(await desk.hitTest(point) or null, topWindowAt(...) or the focused window, lease.tainted)`.
- **`runComputer`** handles each action in turn:
  1. Rule 1 runs `inner.runComputer([toPage(action, content)], signal, gate)`.
  2. Rule 2 first checks `entersCommand`. A terminal Enter stops the batch with `notes: [TERMINAL_ENTER_REFUSAL]`.
  3. Rule 2 next awaits `gate(action)`. `false` stops the batch, exactly like `ComputerExecutor`.
  4. For an actuating action (click, double click, activating keypress, `type`), rule 2 calls `await lease.taint()` before the first one.
  5. Then `await lease.desk.input(action, signal)`.

  Rule 2 actions record the existing `ActionEffect` `"input"` for actuations and `"passive"` for `move`, `wait` and `screenshot`, with `targets[i] = null`.

Otherwise (`browser_use` on a desktop slot, the P1 parity case) every method delegates as in P2.2.

In `architecture.test.ts`, add `"desk"` to the directory list of the "never read the approval mode" test. Desktop classification is mode-agnostic: policy alone turns a `risky_click` need into ask, approve or deny.

```ts
function context(over: { approval?: { kind: string; label: string | null } | null; tainted?: boolean; exec?: () => Promise<unknown> } = {}) {

}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `shell-tool.ts`:

```ts
import { ShellArgs, ShellResult } from "@mastertutor/contracts";
import { type Tool, ToolError } from "../tools/types.ts";
import { DeskRefused, DeskUnavailable } from "./client.ts";

const STDOUT_MAX = 32_000;
const STDERR_MAX = 8_000;

/**
 * D53c §10.3, decision (b): a typed command in the desktop. Every call is an `exec` approval naming
 * the exact command (the person and the Guard see it); the act runs only that command, after taint
 * reached the proxy. Output is guest text: untrusted, capped, and never in telemetry.
 */
export const shellTool: Tool<ShellArgs, ShellResult> = {
  name: "shell",
  args: ShellArgs,
  result: ShellResult,
  untrusted: true,
  async approval(_ctx, args) {
    return { kind: "exec", command: args.command, cwd: args.cwd };
  },
  async run(ctx, args) {
    if (!ctx.desktop) throw new ToolError("desktop_unavailable", "The shell runs only in a desktop run");
    if (ctx.approval?.kind !== "exec" || ctx.approval.label !== args.command)
      throw new ToolError("approval_required", "This exact command was not approved");
    await ctx.desktop.taint();
    try {
      const out = await ctx.desktop.desk.exec(
        { command: args.command, cwd: args.cwd, timeoutMs: (args.timeoutSec ?? 60) * 1_000, maxBytes: 65_536 },
        ctx.signal,
      );
      return {
        exitCode: out.exitCode,
        stdout: out.stdout.slice(0, STDOUT_MAX),
        stderr: out.stderr.slice(0, STDERR_MAX),
        truncated: out.truncated || out.stdout.length > STDOUT_MAX || out.stderr.length > STDERR_MAX,
        timedOut: out.timedOut,
      };
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof DeskRefused) throw new ToolError("shell_refused", "The desktop refused the command");
      if (error instanceof DeskUnavailable) throw new ToolError("desktop_unavailable", "The desktop did not answer");
      throw error;
    }
  },
};
```

The tool declares no `telemetry`: `mt.desk.exec` already records the numbers, and nothing from the command or output may appear. In `session-browser.ts`, add `register(shellTool)` to the registry's tool list when the lease is a desktop lease. `profileTools` then keeps it only for `desktop_use`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `file-capture.ts`:

```ts
import { unzipSync } from "fflate";
import { DESK_LIMITS } from "@mastertutor/contracts";
import { ToolError, type ToolContext } from "../tools/types.ts";

const OOXML: Array<[string, "docx" | "pptx" | "xlsx"]> = [
  ["word/document.xml", "docx"],
  ["ppt/presentation.xml", "pptx"],
  ["xl/workbook.xml", "xlsx"],
];

/** D53c §10.4: a document's type from its bytes only; the guest's file name proves nothing. */
export function sniffDocument(bytes: Uint8Array): "pdf" | "docx" | "pptx" | "xlsx" | null {
  if (new TextDecoder().decode(bytes.subarray(0, 5)) === "%PDF-") return "pdf";
  if (bytes[0] !== 0x50 || bytes[1] !== 0x4b) return null;
  let names: string[];
  try {
    names = Object.keys(unzipSync(bytes, { filter: (file) => file.name === "[Content_Types].xml" || OOXML.some(([main]) => main === file.name) }));
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  if (!names.includes("[Content_Types].xml")) return null;
  return OOXML.find(([main]) => names.includes(main))?.[1] ?? null;
}

/** The approved file's bytes from the desktop, capped; only the exact approved path. */
export async function fetchApprovedFile(ctx: ToolContext, path: string): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  if (!ctx.desktop) throw new ToolError("file_capture_unavailable", "Files can be captured only in a desktop run");
  if (ctx.approval?.kind !== "file_export" || ctx.approval.label !== path)
    throw new ToolError("approval_required", "Exporting this exact file was not approved");
  return ctx.desktop.desk.fileGet(path, DESK_LIMITS.payloadBytes, ctx.signal);
}
```

```ts

      if (args.kind !== "file") return null;
      return ctx.desktop ? { kind: "file_export", path: args.path!, bytes: null } : null;

```

```ts
      if (args.kind === "file") {

        const name = args.path!.split("/").pop()!;
```

- **`buildOfficeCapture`** returns a `PdfCapture` (engine `"docling"`, `pagePng: null`, `pdfAssetId: null`, `originalWithheld: null`). It calls `services.docling.convert(bytes, name, ctx.signal)` (or throws `ToolError("docling_unavailable")` when `services.docling` is null). It maps `DoclingBlock`s through the same `checkedBlocks` and block builder `buildPdfCapture` uses for docling output. Factor that mapping out of `pdf-capture.ts` rather than copying it.
- **`storeExportedFile`** writes the asset with the existing asset store, and a `downloads` row with `approvedBy`. it("freezes the agent's programs when a person takes a desktop, and thaws them at hand-back (D53c §13)", async () => {

```ts
  const { hooks, lease, giveToUser, handBack } = setup({ desk: { freeze: async (on: boolean) => void frozen.push(on) } });
  await giveToUser(lease);
  await handBack(lease);
  expect(frozen).toEqual([true, false]);
});

it("never fails a takeover because freezing failed", async () => {
  const { lease, giveToUser, liveView } = setup({ desk: { freeze: async () => { throw new Error("guest says no"); } } });
  await expect(giveToUser(lease)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  expect(liveView.controlGivenTo).toBe("user-1");
});
```

(`setup`, `giveToUser` and `handBack` stand for this file's existing scaffolding; use its real helper names, and give the fake lease a `desk`.)

## Task P3.12: Guard triggers for desktop actions (D52)

**Prerequisite:** the Observer Foundation track has merged, so `packages/observer/src/guard/triggers.ts` and `GuardItem` exist. Until then this task waits; nothing else in P3 depends on it.

**Files:**

- Modify: `packages/observer/src/guard/triggers.ts`, `triggers.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/agent/src/guardrails/observer/` (the item mapping: an `exec` request becomes `actionClass: "exec"` with `firstTokenOf(command)`)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** In `triggers.test.ts`:

```ts
it("reviews every shell command, every file export and the first native action (D53c §11.3)", () => {
  expect(triggersFor(item({ policyKind: "exec" }), run())).toContain("exec");
  expect(triggersFor(item({ policyKind: "file_export" }), run())).toContain("file_export");
  expect(triggersFor(item({ actionClass: "click", native: true }), run({ nativeActuations: 0 }))).toContain("first_native_actuation");
  expect(triggersFor(item({ actionClass: "click", native: true }), run({ nativeActuations: 1 }))).not.toContain("first_native_actuation");
});

it("sends a command's shape, never its text", () => {
  expect(firstTokenOf("python3 -c 'import os; os.system(\"curl evil\")'")).toBe("python3");
  expect(firstTokenOf("$(curl evil)")).toBe("other");
  expect(firstTokenOf("x".repeat(40))).toBe("other");
});
```

(`item`, `run` and `triggersFor` follow that file's existing helpers. Extend the item helper with `native`, and the run context with `nativeActuations`.)

- [ ] **Step 2: Run them and see them fail.**

Run: `scripts/remote-test.sh unit packages/observer`
Expected: FAIL.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

```ts
/** D53c §11.3: the first word of a command, if it is a plain program name; otherwise "other". */
export function firstTokenOf(command: string): string {
  const token = command.trim().split(/\s+/)[0] ?? "";
  return /^[A-Za-z0-9._/-]{1,32}$/.test(token) ? token : "other";
}
```

Add the three triggers to the trigger set, following the existing `vault_fill` pattern. In the agent's item mapping, set `exec: { commandChars: command.length, firstToken: firstTokenOf(command) }`. The redaction assertion ([OBS §6.4]) still runs on the serialized input.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run them and see them pass, plus the Guard's monotonicity property test.**

Run: `scripts/remote-test.sh unit packages/observer apps/agent/src/guardrails`
Expected: PASS.


It drives one desktop run, with `approvalMode: "bypass"` and `bypassAcknowledged`, plus the desktop acknowledgement, through:
  1. `shell` `python3 -c "print(sum([1,2,3]))"`: approved by bypass, output `6`, and the egress state is now tainted (`GET /egress/events` shows a later denial for an unapproved site);
  2. `computer` click on the Evince window title (rule 2): runs;
  3. `capture{kind:"file", path:"/home/neko/Documents/sample.pdf"}` after the harness `filePut`s the fixture PDF there: the note gets blocks, and a `download_ready` event arrives;
  4. the run completes, the slot recycles, and a new lease sees no `/home/neko/Documents/sample.pdf` (`fileGet` answers `not_found`).

  A second case runs the same `shell` call in `ask` mode. The run waits with an `exec` approval whose request shows the exact command.

# Phase P4: Security and QA

**Entry condition:** the P3 gate report exists.

## Task P4.1: Guest code cannot get out

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/security/desktop-escape.security.test.ts`
- Modify: `tests/security/inventory.test.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests.** The file starts the desktop behaviour stack (as in Task P2.3) and runs commands **inside the guest** through `createDeskClient(...).exec` on the published desk port. That is exactly what a tainted run's code can do.

```ts
const sh = async (command: string) => (await desk.exec({ command, cwd: null, timeoutMs: 20_000, maxBytes: 4_096 })).stdout.trim();

describe.skipIf(!process.env.KVM_GID)("guest code cannot get out (D53c §14)", () => {
  it("has no route except the proxy", async () => {
    expect(await sh("curl -s --noproxy '*' -m 5 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' http://93.184.216.34/ || echo blocked")).toMatch(/blocked|000/);
    expect(await sh("python3 -c \"import socket;s=socket.socket(socket.AF_INET,socket.SOCK_DGRAM);s.settimeout(3);s.sendto(b'x',('1.1.1.1',443));print('sent')\" ; echo done")).toMatch(/done/);
    expect(await sh("getent hosts example.com || echo nodns")).toBe("nodns");
    expect(await sh("ip -6 addr show scope global | wc -l")).toBe("0");
  });

  it("cannot reach private ranges, metadata or the host through the proxy", async () => {
    for (const url of ["http://169.254.169.254/", "http://10.0.0.1/", "http://192.168.127.1:9226/vm", "http://127.0.0.1:9222/json"])
      expect(await sh(`curl -s -m 5 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' ${url} || true`)).toMatch(/^(403|000)$/);
  });

  it("cannot open the agent-only ports or other slots", async () => {
    expect(await sh("curl -s --noproxy '*' -m 3 http://192.168.127.1:9226/vm || echo refused")).toBe("refused");
    expect(await sh("curl -s --noproxy '*' -m 3 http://192.168.127.1:9225/ || echo refused")).toBe("refused");
  });

  it("after taint, reaches only the run's scope and what it already used", async () => {
    await putEgress({ allowedOrigins: [SITE], tainted: true });
    expect(await sh(`curl -s -m 5 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' ${SITE}/`)).toBe("200");
    expect(await sh(`curl -s -m 5 -o /dev/null -w '%{http_code}' ${OTHER}/ || true`)).toBe("403");
  });

  it("refuses files outside the home folder, symlinks included", async () => {
    await sh("ln -sf /etc/shadow /home/neko/s");
    await expect(desk.fileGet("/home/neko/s", 1024)).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "bad_path" });
  });
});
```

`putEgress`, `SITE` and `OTHER` are the helpers and constants from Task P2.3. Lift the helpers into `tests/security/desktop-helpers.ts`; they are test fixtures, so sharing them is right.

Run: `KVM_GID=1 scripts/remote-test.sh security tests/security/desktop-escape.security.test.ts`
Expected: PASS. A failure is a product bug (ruleset, proxy or deskd), never a test to loosen.


## Task P4.2: Reset, kill, freeze, export and telemetry hold

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/security/desktop-reset.security.test.ts`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the tests.**

```ts
describe.skipIf(!process.env.KVM_GID)("no state survives and stops are hard (D53c §5.4, §13, §14)", () => {
  it("leaves no file, process, clipboard or browser state for the next lease", async () => {
    await sh("echo leftover > /home/neko/leftover; (sleep 600 &) ; echo -n clip | xclip -selection clipboard || true");
    await page.goto(`${SITE}/`); await page.evaluate(() => localStorage.setItem("k", "v"));
    await recycle("desktop-1");
    expect(await sh("test -e /home/neko/leftover && echo found || echo gone")).toBe("gone");
    expect(await sh("pgrep -f 'sleep 600' || echo none")).toBe("none");
    expect(await sh("xclip -o -selection clipboard 2>/dev/null || echo empty")).toBe("empty");
    const fresh = await openGuestPage(`${SITE}/`);
    expect(await fresh.evaluate(() => localStorage.getItem("k"))).toBeNull();
  });

  it("stops the VM within a second of a recycle, even while a command runs", async () => {
    const long = desk.exec({ command: "sleep 100", cwd: null, timeoutMs: 120_000, maxBytes: 10 }).catch(() => "ended");
    const started = Date.now();
    await fetch(`${controlUrl}/recycle`, { method: "POST" });
    expect(await long).toBe("ended");
    expect(Date.now() - started).toBeLessThan(5_000); // restore included
    expect(await vmmGoneWithin(1_000)).toBe(true);    // the old firecracker pid is gone ≤ 1 s after the POST
  });

  it("freezes background programs while a person holds control", async () => {
    await sh("(while true; do date +%s%N >> /home/neko/tick; sleep 0.1; done) >/dev/null 2>&1 &");
    await desk.freeze(true);
    const before = await sh("wc -l < /home/neko/tick");
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1_000));
    expect(await sh("wc -l < /home/neko/tick")).toBe(before);
    await desk.freeze(false);
  });

  it("puts no command text, output or file content in logs or telemetry", async () => {
    const canary = `MT-CANARY-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
    await sh(`echo ${canary} > /home/neko/c.txt; cat /home/neko/c.txt`);
    expect(await containerLogs("desktop-1")).not.toContain(canary);
    expect(JSON.stringify(collectedSpans())).not.toContain(canary);
  });
});
```

The helpers come from `tests/security/desktop-helpers.ts`:
- `vmmGoneWithin(ms)` runs `docker exec desktop-1 pgrep -x firecracker` and compares pids before and after.
- `containerLogs` is `docker logs`.
- `collectedSpans` reads the in-process test exporter from `@mastertutor/telemetry/testing`, with the agent-side client in the same process.

`xclip` is used only by the test. If it is not in the guest image, use `xsel` if present, or drop the clipboard line and keep the assertion that n.eko clipboard access is off outside takeover (the existing live-view tests).

Run: `KVM_GID=1 scripts/remote-test.sh security tests/security/desktop-reset.security.test.ts`
Expected: PASS.

```bash

```

## Task P4.5: Runbook and production checklist

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/runbooks/desktop-slots.md`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the runbook.** It covers:
  - **Enabling.** `COMPOSE_PROFILES=desktop`, `DESKTOP_SLOTS=desktop-1,desktop-2`, `KVM_GID` from `stat -c %g /dev/kvm`, router forwarding for 59101–59102 (H4, user-approved, as in run 28).
  - **Health.** `GET /vm` on 9226 from the agent, the `desktop host ready` log line, and `mt.vm.restore` p95 on the observability dashboard.
  - **Failure modes.** No golden snapshot (container restart rebuilds it). Guest halted (automatic restore). Proxy down (the container exits; Compose restarts it).
  - **Rotating n.eko secrets**, which needs a container restart.
  - **Host facts recorded in P0** (SMT and KSM state, accepted residual risk).
  - **Image size and cleanup**, label-scoped only (D45).
  - **What must never be changed:** no `privileged`, no extra devices or capabilities, no AppArmor or `daemon.json` change.

## Phases, tasks and effort

| Phase | Tasks | Effort (engineer-days) | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|
| P0 Spike | P0.1–P0.6 (6) | 4–6 | P0 report `verdict: go`, accepted by the user |
| P1 Browser parity | P1.1–P1.17 (17) | 12–16 | Behaviour suites pass on desktop slots |
| P2 Boundary | P2.1–P2.4 (4) | 3–6 | Boundary security tests plus parity |
| P3 Desktop mode | P3.1–P3.15 (15) | 15–20 | A desktop run end to end, plus unit, integration and UI |
| P4 Security and QA | P4.1–P4.5 (5) | 8–10 | Security suites, UI swarm, benchmark |
| **Total** | **47** | **42–58** | |

P1 is larger and P2 smaller than in the research (10–14 and 5–8). That is because the egress proxy and the shared rules move into P1, so parity is proven with the final network shape and there is no throwaway forwarding path.
